//! A family command expands to current individual, source-bound records.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#18-wave-26-security-decisions-dx9 — review questions lead, and gates never enter the audience family.
use super::*;
use snapback4_lang::Diagnostic;

pub(super) fn audience(code: &str) -> bool {
    !gated(code) && matches!(code, "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE" | "N_BROAD_READ")
}
pub(super) fn family(notes: &[Diagnostic], identities: &[(String,String,String)]) -> Option<String> {
    let mut members = notes.iter().zip(identities).filter(|(note,_)| audience(&note.code))
        .map(|(_, (id,source,_))| (id,source)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    if members.is_empty() { return None; }
    members.sort();
    Some(format!("audience:{}", hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&(ANALYZER,members)).unwrap())))
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn count(dispositions: &[Disposition]) -> usize {
    let audience = dispositions.iter().any(|d| !d.acknowledged && d.family.is_some());
    usize::from(audience) + dispositions.iter().filter(|d| !d.acknowledged && d.family.is_none()
        && crate::note_rank::tier(&d.code)==crate::note_rank::Tier::Review).count()
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn print(dispositions: &[Disposition], notes: &[Diagnostic]) {print!("{}",render(dispositions,notes));}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn render(dispositions: &[Disposition], notes: &[Diagnostic]) -> String {
    use std::fmt::Write;
    let mut output=String::new();
    let mut ordered=notes.iter().zip(dispositions).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004#165-review-clarification-exact-detector-and-command-boundaries — grouping must preserve gate/authority priority.
    ordered.sort_by_key(|(n,d)|(if d.gated&&!d.acknowledged {0} else if d.witness.is_some(){1}else{2},crate::note_rank::summary_order(&n.code),n.site.file.clone(),n.site.line,n.site.col));
    let members=ordered.iter().filter(|(_,d)|d.family.is_some()&&!d.acknowledged&&d.witness.is_none()).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let mut family_output=String::new();
    if let Some((_,first))=members.first().copied() {
        let codes=members.iter().map(|(n,_)|n.code.as_str()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"decision audience ({codes}): Are these {} row/column audiences, roles and lifecycle states intended?",members.len()).unwrap();
        for (n,_) in &members {
            // The expanded branch cross-product stays in JSON. One question
            // names the source subject without repeating every equivalent path.
            let subject = n.message.split(" matches or weakens").next().unwrap_or(&n.message)
                .split(": ").next().unwrap_or(&n.message);
            writeln!(&mut family_output,"  ? {}: Is {} intended?",n.site,subject.trim_end_matches('.')).unwrap();
        }
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"  Intended: snapback4 check --ack-security {} --reason '<spec rationale>'",first.family.as_deref().unwrap()).unwrap();
        writeln!(&mut family_output,"  Narrower: restrict the listed rules; declare independent intent; run snapback4 test. Full audiences: check --json.").unwrap();
    }
    let mut family_printed=false;
    for (n,d) in ordered {
        if d.acknowledged {if d.witness.is_none() {writeln!(&mut output,"  Witness: this code has no replay witness available; acknowledgment records specification rationale, not validation").unwrap();} if let Some(w)=&d.witness {writeln!(&mut output,"  Witness recorded: {}",w["summary"].as_str().unwrap_or("see check --json")).unwrap();} writeln!(&mut output,"security reviewed {} {}: {}",d.id,d.code,d.reason.as_deref().unwrap_or("")).unwrap(); continue; }
        if d.family.is_some() {
            if d.witness.is_some() { /* witnessed audience questions render individually below */ }
            else { if !family_printed {output.push_str(&family_output);family_printed=true;}
            continue; }
        }
        if !d.gated && d.witness.is_none() && crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)>crate::note_rank::Tier::Review { continue; }
        let subject=n.message.split(": ").next().unwrap_or(&n.message);
        let concise=match n.code.as_str() {
            "N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED" if d.witness.as_ref().is_some_and(witness::concrete)=>format!("{subject}: the replayed {} effect via {} proceeds without this approval",d.witness.as_ref().unwrap()["effect"].as_str().unwrap_or("write"),d.witness.as_ref().unwrap()["operation"].as_str().unwrap_or("operation")),
            "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE"=>format!("{subject}: should operation results/refusals expose protected or projected-out columns to every admitted caller"),
            _=>n.message.trim_end_matches(['.', '?']).to_owned(),
        };
        let concise=concise.replace("insert/upsert", "insert, or edit an existing row via upsert");
        let question=concise.trim_end_matches(['.', '?']);
        writeln!(&mut output,"decision {} at {}: {question}; is this intended?",d.code,d.site).unwrap();
        if let Some(witness)=&d.witness {writeln!(&mut output,"  Witness: {}",witness["summary"].as_str().unwrap_or("concrete campaign behavior; check --json for replay")).unwrap();}
        else if matches!(n.code.as_str(),"N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED"|"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"|"N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT"|"N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY") {writeln!(&mut output,"  Witness: none obtained in this bounded campaign; `check --review` searches for concrete behavior; this remains a specification question").unwrap();}
        if d.witness.is_none() && !matches!(n.code.as_str(),"N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED"|"N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"|"N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT"|"N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY") {
            writeln!(&mut output,"  Witness: unavailable for this code; this is specification advice, not a validated behavior").unwrap();
        }
        let repair=match n.code.as_str() {
            "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE"=>"restrict result/refusal audiences with column rules and independent intent; run snapback4 test",
            "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT"=>"validate the recipient's current role and relationship; caller authority alone is insufficient",
            _=>n.rewrite.as_str(),
        };
        writeln!(&mut output,"  Repair: {repair}").unwrap();
        let rationale=d.witness.as_ref().filter(|v|witness::concrete(v)).map(witness::reason_template).unwrap_or_else(||"<spec rationale>".into());
        writeln!(&mut output,"  Intended: snapback4 check --ack-security {} --reason '{}'{}",d.id,rationale,if d.gated {" (required before check can pass)"}else{""}).unwrap();
    }
    output
}

/// One mode selector for the CLI and captured-analyzer format replays.
pub(in crate::verbs) fn render_mode(dispositions:&[Disposition],notes:&[Diagnostic],review:bool)->String {
    if !review {
        let mut output=String::new();
        for (d,n) in dispositions.iter().zip(notes) {
            if d.gated&&!d.acknowledged || d.recorded {
                output.push_str(&render(&[d.clone()],&[n.clone()]));
            } else if !d.acknowledged && let Some(w)=&d.witness {
                let rationale=if witness::concrete(w) {witness::reason_template(w)} else {"<spec rationale>".into()};
                output.push_str(&format!("fix question {} at {}: {}; is this intended?\n  Witness: {} — repair or check --ack-security {} --reason '{}' (doesn't block)\n",d.code,d.site,n.message.trim_end_matches(['.','?']),w["compactSummary"].as_str().or_else(||w["summary"].as_str()).unwrap_or("see check --json for replay"),d.id,rationale));
            }
        }
        return output;
    }
    let selected=dispositions.iter().zip(notes).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    render(&selected.iter().map(|(d,_)|(*d).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>(),&selected.iter().map(|(_,n)|(*n).clone()).collect::<Vec<_>>())
}
#[cfg(test)]mod tests {
 use super::*;
 #[test]fn authority_and_gates_precede_one_complete_audience_family(){
  let codes=["N_BROAD_READ","N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION","N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE","N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"];
  let notes=codes.iter().map(|code|Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),*code,"subject: review authority","repair enforcement")).collect::<Vec<_>>();
  let dispositions=codes.iter().map(|code|Disposition{family:audience(code).then(||"audience:family".into()),id:(*code).into(),code:(*code).into(),site:"schema.q:1:1".into(),gated:gated(code),acknowledged:false,recorded:false,reason:None,witness:None,scope:"test".into()}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
  let text=render(&dispositions,&notes);
  assert!(text.find("decision N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE").unwrap()<text.find("decision N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION").unwrap(),"{text}");
  assert!(text.find("decision N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION").unwrap()<text.find("decision audience").unwrap(),"{text}");
  assert_eq!(text.matches("decision audience").count(),1,"{text}");
  assert!(text.contains("Are these 2 row/column audiences")&&text.contains("audience:family"),"{text}");
 }
 #[test]fn every_gate_is_one_decision_and_never_a_family_member(){
  for code in snapback4_lang::security::GATED_NOTE_CODES {
   assert!(!audience(code));
   let n=Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),*code,"explicit review required","repair enforcement");
   let d=Disposition{family:None,id:"id".into(),code:(*code).into(),site:"schema.q:1:1".into(),gated:true,acknowledged:false,recorded:false,reason:None,witness:None,scope:"test".into()};
   let text=render(&[d],&[n]);assert_eq!(text.matches(code).count(),1,"{text}");assert!(text.contains("is this intended?")&&text.contains("Repair:")&&text.contains("Intended:"));
  }
  for code in ["N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY","N_CALLER_TIME","N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE","N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED","E_SECURITY_REVIEW"] {assert!(!audience(code));}
 }
}

#[cfg(test)]mod sec10_replay {
 use super::*;
 fn diagnostic(n:&serde_json::Value)->Diagnostic {Diagnostic::new(&snapback4_lang::Site::new(n["site"]["file"].as_str().unwrap(),n["site"]["line"].as_u64().unwrap() as u32,n["site"]["col"].as_u64().unwrap() as u32),n["code"].as_str().unwrap(),n["message"].as_str().unwrap(),n["rewrite"].as_str().unwrap())}
 fn disposition(n:&Diagnostic,gated:bool)->Disposition {Disposition{family:None,id:n.code.clone(),code:n.code.clone(),site:n.site.to_string(),gated,acknowledged:false,recorded:false,reason:None,scope:"captured SEC10 source-bound decision".into(),witness:None}}
 #[test]fn captured_sec10_codes_keep_every_gate_and_hide_advisories_by_default(){
  let codes:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_str(include_str!("../../../tests/fixtures/dx11/sec10-codes.json")).unwrap();
  for n in codes.as_object().unwrap().values(){let n=diagnostic(n);let d=disposition(&n,false);assert!(render_mode(&[d],&[n.clone()],false).is_empty());let d=disposition(&n,true);let output=render_mode(&[d],&[n.clone()],false);assert!(output.contains(&n.code)&&output.contains("Intended:"),"{output}");}
 }
 /// Opt-in measurement on all 50 actual SEC10 structured reports. No analyzer
 /// or source is modified: only the production presentation function runs.
 #[test]fn captured_sec10_measurement(){
  let Ok(path)=std::env::var("SNAPBACK4_DX11_SEC10_REPORTS")else{return;};
  let rows:serde_json::Value=serde_json::from_slice(&std::fs::read(path).unwrap()).unwrap();let mut measured=Vec::new();
  for row in rows.as_array().unwrap(){
   let notes=row["notes"].as_array().unwrap().iter().map(diagnostic).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   let ds=row["dispositions"].as_array().unwrap().iter().zip(&notes).map(|(d,n)|disposition(n,d["gated"]==true)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
   let gate_count=ds.iter().filter(|d|d.gated).count();
   let project=crate::project::Project{root:std::path::PathBuf::from(format!("/tmp/dx11-replay/sec10/{}",row["lane"].as_str().unwrap()))};
   let sources=project.sources().unwrap();let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&sources);assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty());
   let mut report=snapback4_lang::security::analyze_rules(&bound);report.notes=notes.clone();
   let advice=ds.iter().filter(|d|!d.gated&&crate::note_rank::tier(&d.code)==crate::note_rank::Tier::Review).count();
   let verdict=crate::presentation::check_verdict(gate_count>0,&crate::verbs::table_counts(&bound.schema),bound.schema.synced().count(),bound.programs.len(),if gate_count==0{"compiled"}else{"security review required"},gate_count,0,advice,0,row["noteCount"].as_u64().unwrap_or(0) as usize,row["ms"].as_f64().unwrap(),crate::verbs::init::client_setup_hint(&project.root),"");
   let text=format!("{verdict}\n{}\n{}",crate::verbs::security::enforced_text(&report,&bound),render_mode(&ds,&notes,false));
   let lines=text.lines().count();if gate_count==0{assert!(lines<=5);}
   measured.push(serde_json::json!({"lane":row["lane"],"exit":if gate_count==0{0}else{1},"lines":lines,"chars":text.chars().count(),"gates":gate_count}));
  }
  if let Ok(path)=std::env::var("SNAPBACK4_DX11_RENDER_OUTPUT"){std::fs::write(path,serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&measured).unwrap()).unwrap();}
 }
}

#[cfg(test)] mod dx13_tests {
 use super::*;
 #[test] fn witnessed_questions_are_default_visible_and_unwitnessable_ack_is_explicit() {
  let n=Diagnostic::new(&Default::default(),"N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED","jobs.approved: transitions do not inspect approval","add guard");
  let mut d=Disposition{family:None,id:"question-id".into(),code:n.code.clone(),site:n.site.to_string(),gated:false,acknowledged:false,recorded:false,reason:None,witness:Some(serde_json::json!({"summary":"advance as staff -> ok; approved=false"})),scope:"test".into()};
  let output=render_mode(&[d.clone()],&[n.clone()],false);assert!(output.contains("Witness: advance")&&output.contains("fix question")&&output.contains("doesn't block"));
  d.code="N_CHILD_EVIDENCE_PRESENCE".into();d.witness=None;

  d.acknowledged=true;d.recorded=true;d.reason=Some("spec rationale".into());

 }

//! Individually justified dispositions; current evidence is never runtime authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#162-narrow-gates-and-individual-dispositions — bulk compiler-note review cannot clear security gates.
use super::*;
use serde::{Serialize,Deserialize};
use sha2::{Digest,Sha256};
const ANALYZER:&str="sec10-wave28-dx11-1";
mod binding;
pub(super) mod witness;
mod decisions;
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize,PartialEq,Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct File{version:u32,entries:BTreeMap<String,Entry>}
#[derive(Serialize,Deserialize,PartialEq,Eq)]
#[serde(deny_unknown_fields)]
struct Entry{sources:String,analyzer:String,reason:String,#[serde(default,skip_serializing_if="Option::is_none")] witness:Option<serde_json::Value>}
#[derive(Clone,Serialize)]
pub(super) struct Disposition{pub family:Option<String>,pub id:String,pub code:String,pub site:String,pub gated:bool,pub acknowledged:bool,pub recorded:bool,pub reason:Option<String>,pub scope:String,pub witness:Option<serde_json::Value>}
pub(super) fn gated(code:&str)->bool{snapback4_lang::security::gated_note(code)}
fn hash(bytes:&[u8])->String{format!("{:x}",Sha256::digest(bytes))}
fn digest(s:&str)->bool{s.len()==64&&s.bytes().all(|b|b.is_ascii_hexdigit())}
pub(super) fn review(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>)->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    review_inner(project,sources,notes,ack,false,&[])
}
/// Read-only decisions for a caller-validated immutable saved-source selection.
pub(super) fn review_snapshot(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic])->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    review_inner(project,sources,notes,None,true,&[])
}
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#11-wave-27-precision-and-workflow-conservation — one decision snapshot binds replay priority and disposition.
pub(super) struct ReviewSnapshot{file:File}
impl ReviewSnapshot{
    pub(super) fn capture(project:&Project)->Result<Self,String>{Ok(Self{file:read_file(project)?})}
    pub(super) fn has_decisions(&self)->bool{!self.file.entries.is_empty()}
    pub(super) fn dispositions(&self,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic])->Vec<Disposition>{self.dispositions_with_evidence(sources,notes,&[])}
    pub(super) fn dispositions_with_evidence(&self,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],evidence:&[Option<serde_json::Value>])->Vec<Disposition>{dispositions(&self.file,notes,witness::identities(notes,sources,evidence),&Default::default(),evidence)}
    pub(super) fn ensure_current(&self,project:&Project)->Result<(),String>{
        if read_file(project)?!=self.file{return Err("security review decisions changed during deployment preflight; rerun this deploy command to replay current individual decisions".into());}Ok(())
    }
}
fn read_file(project:&Project)->Result<File,String>{
    let file:File=match std::fs::read(project.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json")){
        Ok(bytes)=>serde_json::from_slice(&bytes).map_err(|_|"invalid snapback/security-reviews.json; expected version 1 with per-note source/analyzer/reason records".to_string())?,
        Err(e) if e.kind()==std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound=>File{version:1,entries:BTreeMap::new()},
        Err(e)=>return Err(format!("cannot read security dispositions: {e}")),
    };
    if file.version!=1||file.entries.iter().any(|(key,e)|!digest(key)||!digest(&e.sources)||e.reason.trim().is_empty()||e.analyzer.is_empty()){return Err("invalid security disposition version/digest/reason".into());}
    Ok(file)
}
pub(super) fn review_evidence(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>,evidence:&[Option<serde_json::Value>])->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    review_inner(project,sources,notes,ack,false,evidence)
}
fn review_inner(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],ack:Option<(&str,&str)>,snapshot:bool,evidence:&[Option<serde_json::Value>])->Result<Vec<Disposition>,String>{
    if !snapshot&&project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed during security review; rerun check".into());}
    let identities=witness::identities(notes,sources,evidence);
    let path=project.schema_dir().join("security-reviews.json");
    // Keep the lock inode in ignored generated output. Do not unlink a lock
    // while another process can be waiting on that inode. Kernel ownership
    // releases it on exit, including an interrupted writer.
    let _writer_lock=if ack.is_some(){
        let directory=project.schema_dir().join("generated");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&directory).map_err(|e|format!("cannot create security review lock directory: {e}"))?;
        let lock=std::fs::OpenOptions::new().create(true).truncate(false).read(true).write(true).open(directory.join("security-reviews.lock")).map_err(|e|format!("cannot open security review lock: {e}"))?;
        lock.lock().map_err(|e|format!("cannot acquire security review lock: {e}"))?;Some(lock)
    }else{None};
    if !snapshot&&project.sources().map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=sources{return Err("Quarry sources changed while waiting for security review; rerun check".into());}
    let mut file=read_file(project)?;
    let mut recorded=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    if let Some((key,reason))=ack{
        if reason.trim().is_empty(){return Err("--reason needs the specification rationale for this individual note".into());}
        // Validate the complete batch against the same captured source/note set.
        // Nothing is published when even one member is stale or unknown.
        let mut selected=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        for key in key.split(',').map(str::trim) {
            if key.starts_with("audience:") {
                let Some(family)=decisions::family(notes,&identities) else {return Err("no current advisory audience family; run check --review --json".into());};
                if family==key {
                    selected.extend(notes.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_,note)|decisions::audience(&note.code)).map(|(i,_)|i));
                } else {
                    let history: BTreeMap<String,Vec<(String,String)>>=std::fs::read(project.schema_dir().join("generated/security-families.json")).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
                    let members=history.get(key).ok_or("stale or unknown audience family: rerun check --review; existing individual decisions are preserved")?;
                    for (id,fingerprint) in members {
                        let index=identities.iter().enumerate().find(|(i,(current,binding,_))|current==id&&binding==fingerprint&&decisions::audience(&notes[*i].code)).map(|(i,_)|i).ok_or("stale audience family member: a relevant rule changed or disappeared; no decisions recorded; run check --review")?;
                        selected.insert(index);
                    }
                }
            } else {
                let index=identities.iter().position(|(id,_,_)|id==key).ok_or_else(||format!("--ack-security {key} is stale, belongs to another source selection, or is not a decision ID. Use securityReviews[].id from `check --review --json` (not a diagnostic code or invariant site); repeated --ack-security ID or comma-separated IDs share one --reason"))?;
                selected.insert(index);
            }
        }
        recorded=selected.clone();
        for index in selected {
            let (id,fingerprint,_)=&identities[index];
            if let Some(value)=evidence.get(index).and_then(Option::as_ref){witness::validate_reason(reason,value)?;}
            file.entries.insert(id.clone(),Entry{sources:fingerprint.clone(),analyzer:ANALYZER.into(),reason:reason.trim().into(),witness:evidence.get(index).cloned().flatten()});
        }
        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("json.{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
        let result=std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec_pretty(&file).unwrap()).and_then(|_|{
            if project.sources()?!=sources{return Err(std::io::Error::other("Quarry sources changed before disposition publication; rerun check"));}
            std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path)
        });
        if let Err(e)=result{let _=std::fs::remove_file(&temporary);return Err(format!("cannot record security disposition: {e}"));}
    }
    if !snapshot{remember_family_identities(project,notes,&identities);}
    Ok(dispositions(&file,notes,identities,&recorded,evidence))
}
// Display history is generated evidence only; it cannot change captured decisions.
pub(super) fn remember_families(project:&Project,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic]){remember_family_identities(project,notes,&binding::identities(notes,sources));}
fn remember_family_identities(project:&Project,notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],identities:&[(String,String,String)]){
    let family=decisions::family(notes,identities);
    if let Some(family)=&family {
        let path=project.schema_dir().join("generated/security-families.json");
        let mut history:BTreeMap<String,Vec<(String,String)>>=std::fs::read(&path).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice(&b).ok()).unwrap_or_default();
        history.insert(family.clone(),identities.iter().zip(notes).filter(|(_,n)|decisions::audience(&n.code)).map(|((id,binding,_),_)|(id.clone(),binding.clone())).collect());
        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
        let _=std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap());
        if std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec(&history).unwrap()).is_ok(){let _=std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path);}let _=std::fs::remove_file(temporary);
    }
}
fn dispositions(file:&File,notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],identities:Vec<(String,String,String)>,recorded:&std::collections::BTreeSet<usize>,evidence:&[Option<serde_json::Value>])->Vec<Disposition>{
    let family=decisions::family(notes,&identities);
    notes.iter().zip(identities).enumerate().map(|(i,(n,(key,fingerprint,scope)))|{let entry=file.entries.get(&key).filter(|e|witness::retained(e,&fingerprint,evidence.get(i).and_then(Option::as_ref)));
        Disposition{family:decisions::audience(&n.code).then(||family.clone()).flatten(),id:key,code:n.code.clone(),site:n.site.to_string(),gated:witness::gate(&n.code,evidence.get(i).and_then(Option::as_ref)),acknowledged:entry.is_some(),recorded:recorded.contains(&i),scope,reason:entry.map(|e|e.reason.clone()),witness:evidence.get(i).cloned().flatten().or_else(||entry.and_then(|e|e.witness.clone()))}
    }).collect()
}

pub(super) fn pending(dispositions:&[Disposition])->bool{dispositions.iter().any(|d|d.gated&&!d.acknowledged)}
pub(super) use decisions::{print,render,render_mode,count as decision_count};

//! Current concrete evidence controls SEC10 gates and witness acknowledgments.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#13-witness-backed-enforcement-and-shipping-precision — a stale rationale never supplies a current witness.
use super::*;
pub(in crate::verbs) fn concrete(value:&serde_json::Value)->bool {
    let Some(history)=value["history"].as_array().filter(|h|!h.is_empty())else{return false;};
    matches!(value["reachability"].as_str(),Some("generated-fixture-trace"|"authored-fixture-trace"))
        && value["candidate"].as_str().is_some_and(|s|!s.is_empty())
        && value["operation"].as_str().is_some_and(|s|!s.is_empty())
        && history.last().is_some_and(|step|step["operation"]==value["operation"]&&step["viewer"]==value["viewer"]&&step["state"]=="sent")
}
fn canonical(value:&serde_json::Value)->serde_json::Value {
    use serde_json::Value;
    use std::collections::BTreeSet;
    fn replace(value:&mut Value,ids:&BTreeMap<String,String>){match value{
        Value::String(s)=>if let Some(alias)=ids.get(s){*s=alias.clone();},
        Value::Object(fields)=>for v in fields.values_mut(){replace(v,ids)},
        Value::Array(xs)=>for v in xs{replace(v,ids)},_=>{}
    }}
    fn sorted(mut value:Value)->Value {
        for key in ["supportingRows","committedRows"]{if let Some(tables)=value.get_mut(key).and_then(Value::as_object_mut){
            for rows in tables.values_mut().filter_map(Value::as_array_mut){rows.sort_by_cached_key(Value::to_string);}
        }}value
    }
    // Refine by the entire typed occurrence context, including reference edges.
    // Individualization resolves any remaining graph symmetry; only an actual
    // transposition automorphism may prune a branch, never a tied local key.
    fn refine(value:&Value,mut colors:BTreeMap<String,String>)->BTreeMap<String,String>{
        loop {
            let signatures=colors.keys().map(|id|{
                let mut labels=colors.clone();labels.insert(id.clone(),"$self".into());
                let mut image=value.clone();replace(&mut image,&labels);
                (id.clone(),(colors[id].clone(),sorted(image).to_string()))
            }).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
            let ranks=signatures.values().cloned().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().into_iter().enumerate().map(|(i,s)|(s,format!("$color-{i:04}"))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
            let next=signatures.into_iter().map(|(id,s)|(id,ranks[&s].clone())).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>();
            let old_count=colors.values().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().len();
            let new_count=next.values().collect::<BTreeSet<_>>().len();
            if old_count==new_count{return next;}colors=next;
        }
    }
    fn search(value:&Value,colors:BTreeMap<String,String>)->Value {
        let colors=refine(value,colors);
        let mut classes=BTreeMap::<String,Vec<String>>::new();
        for (id,color) in &colors{classes.entry(color.clone()).or_default().push(id.clone());}
        let Some(tied)=classes.values().find(|ids|ids.len()>1)else{
            let labels=classes.values().enumerate().map(|(i,ids)|(ids[0].clone(),format!("$generated-{i}"))).collect();
            let mut result=value.clone();replace(&mut result,&labels);return sorted(result);
        };
        let base=sorted(value.clone());let mut representatives=Vec::<String>::new();let mut best=None::<Value>;
        for id in tied{
            let equivalent=representatives.iter().any(|other|{
                let mut swapped=value.clone();replace(&mut swapped,&BTreeMap::from([(id.clone(),other.clone()),(other.clone(),id.clone())]));sorted(swapped)==base
            });
            if equivalent{continue;}representatives.push(id.clone());
            let mut branch=colors.clone();branch.insert(id.clone(),format!("{}-individual",colors[id]));
            let result=search(value,branch);
            if best.as_ref().is_none_or(|old|result.to_string()<old.to_string()){best=Some(result);}
        }best.unwrap()
    }
    let generated=value["generatedIds"].as_array().into_iter().flatten().filter_map(Value::as_str).map(|s|(s.to_owned(),"$color-0000".to_owned())).collect();
    let mut value=value.clone();if let Some(fields)=value.as_object_mut(){for key in ["id","summary","scope","generatedIds","advisory"]{fields.remove(key);}}
    search(&value,generated)
}

pub(in crate::verbs) fn id(value:&serde_json::Value)->String {hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&canonical(value)).unwrap())}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn stamp(mut value:serde_json::Value)->serde_json::Value {value["id"]=id(&value).into();value}
pub(super) fn identities(notes:&[snapback4_lang::Diagnostic],sources:&[(String,String)],evidence:&[Option<serde_json::Value>])->Vec<(String,String,String)> {
    let mut identities=binding::identities(notes,sources);let mut occurrences=BTreeMap::<(String,String),usize>::new();
    for (index,note) in notes.iter().enumerate(){
        if let Some(value)=evidence.get(index).and_then(Option::as_ref).filter(|v|concrete(v)){
            let witness=id(value);let ordinal=occurrences.entry((note.code.clone(),witness.clone())).or_default();
            identities[index]=(hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&("witness-decision-1",&note.code,&witness,*ordinal)).unwrap()),witness,"current replayed witness; source edits alone do not invalidate this decision".into());*ordinal+=1;
        }
    }identities
}
pub(super) fn gate(code:&str,evidence:Option<&serde_json::Value>)->bool {
    // Required-to-optional adoption review is an explicit predecessor contract,
    // not an inferred SEC10 effect. Other questions need current replay evidence.
    gated(code)&&(code=="N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING"||evidence.is_some_and(|value|concrete(value)&&value["advisory"]!=true))
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn reason_template(value:&serde_json::Value)->String {
    let operation=value["operation"].as_str().unwrap_or("operation");
    let answer=if matches!(value["effect"].as_str(),Some("insert"|"upsert")){format!("audience: <intended roles for {operation}>; <spec rationale>")}else{format!("intended effect: <why {operation} is permitted by the spec>")};
    format!("witness {}; permit {} as {}; {answer}",id(value),operation,value["viewer"].as_str().unwrap_or("caller"))
}
pub(super) fn validate_reason(reason:&str,value:&serde_json::Value)->Result<(),String>{
    if !concrete(value){return Ok(());}
    let witness=id(value);let operation=value["operation"].as_str().unwrap();
    let clauses=reason.split(';').map(str::trim).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let permits=format!("permit {operation} as {}",value["viewer"].as_str().unwrap());
    let marker=if matches!(value["effect"].as_str(),Some("insert"|"upsert")){"audience:"}else{"intended effect:"};
    let answers=clauses.iter().filter_map(|clause|clause.strip_prefix(marker).map(str::trim)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let answer=if answers.len()==1{answers[0]}else{""};
    if !clauses.contains(&format!("witness {witness}").as_str())||!clauses.contains(&permits.as_str())||snapback4_core::visible_text::visible_length(answer)<8||answer.contains('<')||answer.contains('>'){
        return Err(format!("this decision must answer the current witness and name its operation; use --reason '{}', replacing the placeholders with the intended audience/effect and specification rationale, or repair the source and rerun check",reason_template(value)));
    }Ok(())
}
pub(super) fn retained(entry:&Entry,fingerprint:&str,evidence:Option<&serde_json::Value>)->bool {
    if let Some(value)=evidence.filter(|v|concrete(v)){
        entry.sources==fingerprint&&entry.witness.as_ref().is_some_and(|old|concrete(old)&&id(old)==id(value))&&validate_reason(&entry.reason,value).is_ok()
    }else{entry.sources==fingerprint&&entry.analyzer==ANALYZER}
}

 fn example(operation:&str)->serde_json::Value {stamp(json!({"operation":operation,"viewer":"account:security-clerk","effect":"insert","candidate":"invariant docs.insert only viewer = 'service:manager'","history":[{"operation":operation,"viewer":"account:security-clerk","state":"sent","args":{"n":1}}],"row":{"n":1},"reachability":"generated-fixture-trace","summary":"current witness","scope":"generated initialization"}))}

  value["history"]=json!([]);assert!(!gate("N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED",Some(&value)));
  let mut value=example("create");value["reachability"]="trusted-seed-only".into();assert!(!gate("N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED",Some(&value)));
  assert!(!gate("N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT",Some(&example("create"))));
 }
 #[test]fn a_witness_requires_an_explicit_creation_audience_answer(){
  let value=example("create");for reason in ["one decision","a specification rationale","audience: clerks create documents"]{assert!(validate_reason(reason,&value).is_err());}
  let reason=format!("witness {}; permit create as account:security-clerk; audience: create is deliberately available to clerks for draft documents",id(&value));assert!(validate_reason(&reason,&value).is_ok());
  assert!(validate_reason(&format!("witness {}; audience: managers are allowed",id(&value)),&value).is_err());
  for permit in ["permit create as account:security-clerk-manager","do not permit create as account:security-clerk","permit create as account:security-clerk is forbidden"]{
   assert!(validate_reason(&format!("witness {}; {permit}; audience: clerks create documents",id(&value)),&value).is_err());
  }
 }
 #[test]fn a_payload_cannot_borrow_the_following_witness_or_permission_clause(){
  for (effect,marker) in [("insert","audience:"),("update","intended effect:")]{
   let mut value=example("create");value["effect"]=effect.into();
   for payload in ["","   ","short","\u{200b}\u{200b}\u{200b}\u{200b}"]{let reason=format!("{marker} {payload}; witness {}; permit create as account:security-clerk",id(&value));assert!(validate_reason(&reason,&value).is_err());}
   let reason=format!("{marker} clerks deliberately perform this specified effect; witness {}; permit create as account:security-clerk",id(&value));assert!(validate_reason(&reason,&value).is_ok());
   let repeated=format!("{reason}; {marker} another conflicting audience");assert!(validate_reason(&repeated,&value).is_err());
  }
 }

# Security invariants

State privacy intent separately from enforcement. Check reports proved, refuted
with a replayed counterexample, or unproved with a reason; passing finite tests
are not proofs.

```quarry
table privateNotes:
  patient: principal
  body: text 1..2000
  sealed: bool default false
  read <- .patient = viewer and .sealed = false
  insert <- .patient = viewer and .sealed = false
  online only
-- Spec: a patient may see only their own note; sealing may narrow availability.
-- Removing an accidental broad branch from the rule must not change this intent.
invariant privateNotes.read only .patient = viewer
invariant privateNotes.body read only .patient = viewer
invariant privateNotes.insert only .patient = viewer
query privateNote(note: privateNotes):
  return privateNotes[note]
mutation writePrivateNote(body: text 1..2000):
  return insert privateNotes { patient: viewer, body }
```

Run `snapback4 check` → `snapback4 test` → `snapback4 audit`. A passing default
check prints its verdict and language-enforcement summary; pending gates stay
visible, as do witnessed questions and actionable write/completeness notes.
Advisories do not block; acknowledgments are optional unless labelled as a gate. Use `snapback4 check --review` for advisory decisions and notes;
`--verbose` retains every reason.
Answer the rule questions from the product specification before editing intent.
The restating advisory names each admitted role and its row conditions: for
example, a coach branch with no assignment check asks whether coaches should
see other coaches' athletes. For every role, answer which rows, which columns,
and which status/consent conditions the spec permits. Include derived values
and downloads: hiding a diagnosis but exposing its category can reveal it.
Write that answer as independent read intent; repair a rule that admits more.
A matching or weaker assertion may still be the right simple owner policy:
keep confirmed intent. The advisory never refuses it or changes its verdict.
Review `snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft`: each role gets a row
question and a question for every column, including roles absent from the rule.
No candidate is accepted automatically. Use table-read intent for a shared row audience; add
column-read intent for distinct privacy boundaries. Assess every role and field
without automatically creating one assertion per question. For each role, identify
a positive grant from the spec or a necessary described workflow before retaining
access to another person's private data. Confirm intentionally shared fields
explicitly; an omitted denial is not a product grant.
Run check, test and audit again after confirming intent;
a refutation means fix the enforcement or the independently verified spec,
without widening intent just to make the result green.

Test synthesizes two users per discovered role/seat and related ownership rows
without fixture files, including password-only apps. These private in-memory
fixtures never open dev doors. Every failure labels its initial fixture origin:
`generated-fixture-trace` means an authored observation from a validated generated
initial state, not proof that app operations created that state. Author-supplied
fixtures retain `authored-fixture-trace`. Both fail acceptance on actual observed
violations; seed-only/synthetic table probes remain non-failing supplemental evidence.
Fixture-free tests report that no authored business cases ran. Finite passing
campaigns never prove security. Check bounds concrete exploration to 512 call/world
reservations; its default verdict never depends on elapsed time. Work exhaustion
stays unproved and points to the full test. Every check and changed deploy also
searches generated question witnesses with a separate deterministic 8192-work
budget. A separate fixed 3072-work current-source search obtains gate evidence
in every check mode and deploy. Without an admitted trace the question stays
advice. Exhausted questions retain an explicit unavailable witness.
`audit --json` retains all column audiences, grant writers, exclusions and uncovered
role/row/effect exposures. Audit returns the permission map with bounded check
evidence; run `test` for the full generated campaign. `audit --all` shows every
reason, without increasing the search. Declaring one effect does not cover the others.

## Reference

`invariant table.column read only P` protects a column value, derived values and value-dependent counts/existence/refusals. Mutation results, implicit final writes and explicit returned row objects are observers too. A query projection alone is not privacy: use column read rules to protect every operation. An authored projection excluding the column does not by itself disclose that value; filtering, ordering or copying it can. Audit JSON includes `column_audiences` (effective row rule plus private-owner gate). Unknown derived and opaque paths remain unproved.

`invariant table.read only P` protects read intent; insert, update and delete
name authority. `invariant table.column write only P` covers writes to a
column (insert, inserted insert-if-absent, update and both upsert arms).
A null creation or a declared literal default (including `false` and `0`) is initialization, not an approval write. Mandatory creation presence needs whole-row insert/write or always intent; non-null/non-default creation still requires P. Tests judge each executed write in a committed mutation, including intermediate writes. `invariant table.column update only P` checks
only edits, retaining old-and-next-image semantics; use it when creation is permitted
to a different role (for example counter staff creating a ticket price). `invariant table always P` states row integrity.
An update policy over `.field` constrains **both old and next images**.
A policy saying status must not be override therefore also restricts editing
a row that was already override, even when changing it away from that status.
For the role authority of one named mutation, use operation-specific intent
(`invariant table.operation only P`) instead. An operator `provision` result excludes only its own executed roster write from authored read intent; later queries and other results remain checked. General next-image-only and
cross-operation transition assertions still need a separate reduction.
Predicates use the existing rule vocabulary: `.field`, `viewer`, `exists`,
comparisons, `and`, `or`, `not`. For a role write use the complete roster probe,
for example `invariant members.role write only exists roles[viewer, role = 'owner']`
in a schema declaring those tables. A declaration never grants authority. Whole-row read intent also protects logical
existence: when occupancy totals are intentionally public but roster identities
are private, declare column read intent for those identities instead. Include
a service seat explicitly when the spec permits it; seats are ordinary checked
viewers, never implicitly exempt.

The implemented proof fragment exhaustively checks independent three-valued
rule predicates. A failed implication is unproved until an engine replay
establishes a counterexample. A schema-valid seeded snapshot is not a claim
that an application operation sequence reaches that state. Required-column
non-null assertions have a separate validation theorem. Read/count/sum/existence obligations are reported separately. A maintained aggregate with bare privacy keys can prove its contributor audience; a broader aggregate can yield a paired present/absent counterexample. Exact server `not before`/`expire at` bounds can prove a temporal read component independently of physical sweeps. General transitions and unsupported expressions remain unproved.
Read-through captures/live parents, complex derived aggregates, raw rule probes,
owned-field update bypasses, cascade/set-null and opaque jobs/workers need
separate reductions. An unresolved path blocks the whole declaration's proof.

Server delivery authorizes against the current server snapshot. A disconnected
device can retain an earlier snapshot until it receives a revocation; even
`until-revoked` cannot know a later server role. Synced current-role intent is
therefore unproved for device access. Retained history follows its leave policy.
These reports exclude physical timing and resource/meter side channels and
explicitly exclude the trusted system-principal boundary. Service seats remain
inside the ordinary-viewer check; there is no implicit seat exemption.

//! Concrete question evidence: hypotheses remain separate from authored intent.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#19-wave-28-output-and-witness-decisions-dx11 — inferred witnesses never become authored refutations.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType;
use snapback4_lang::{
    Diagnostic,
    security::challenges::{self, Counterexample},
};
fn hash(bytes: &[u8]) -> String {
    use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
    format!("{:x}", Sha256::digest(bytes))
}
// Generated values only: retain typed scalars, redact opaque payloads.
fn safe(value: &snapback4_core::Value, kind: Option<&ColumnType>) -> serde_json::Value {
    use snapback4_core::Value;
    match (value, kind.map(ColumnType::required)) {
        (
            _,
            Some(
                ColumnType::Text { .. }
                | ColumnType::Json { .. }
                | ColumnType::Bytes { .. }
                | ColumnType::Image { .. }
                | ColumnType::File { .. }
                | ColumnType::Video { .. },
            ),
        ) => json!("<redacted>"),
        (
            Value::Array(xs),
            Some(ColumnType::List { of, .. } | ColumnType::ListRange { of, .. }),
        ) => json!(xs.iter().map(|v| safe(v, Some(of))).collect::<Vec<_>>()),
        (
            Value::String(_) | Value::Bytes(_) | Value::Row(_) | Value::Map(_) | Value::Array(_),
            None,
        ) => json!("<redacted>"),
        _ => snapback4_core::wire::to_json(value),
    }
}
fn image(bound: &Bound, table: &str, row: &snapback4_core::Value) -> serde_json::Value {
    let snapback4_core::Value::Row(fields) = row else {
        return json!("<redacted>");
    };
    json!(
        fields
            .iter()
            .map(|(name, value)| (
                name,
                safe(
                    value,
                    bound.schema.table(table).and_then(|t| t.columns.get(name))
                )
            ))
            .collect::<BTreeMap<_, _>>()
    )
}
fn actual_args(c: &Counterexample) -> &BTreeMap<String, snapback4_core::Value> {
    if !c.args.is_empty() {
        return &c.args;
    }
    c.history
        .iter()
        .rev()
        .find(|step| step.operation == c.operation && step.viewer == c.viewer)
        .map(|step| &step.args)
        .unwrap_or(&c.args)
}
// Retain the actual trace's transitive data/authority graph, not unrelated
// fixture populations. Helpers are expanded in the bound IR read/write sites.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#13-witness-backed-enforcement-and-shipping-precision — unrelated tables cannot invalidate a witnessed decision.
fn dependencies(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound,inv:&security::Invariant,c:&Counterexample)->std::collections::BTreeSet<String>{
    use snapback4_core::ir;
    let mut tables=security::derived::witness_dependencies(sources,bound,inv);
    fn named(value:&serde_json::Value,bound:&Bound,out:&mut std::collections::BTreeSet<String>){match value{
        serde_json::Value::Object(fields)=>for (key,value) in fields{
            if matches!(key.as_str(),"table"|"source"|"target"|"parent")&&let Some(name)=value.as_str().filter(|name|bound.schema.tables.contains_key(*name)){out.insert(name.into());}
            named(value,bound,out);
        },serde_json::Value::Array(values)=>for value in values{named(value,bound,out)},_=>{}
    }}
    for operation in std::iter::once(c.operation.as_str()).chain(c.history.iter().map(|step|step.operation.as_str())){
        let Some(program)=bound.programs.iter().find(|p|p.program.name==operation)else{return bound.schema.tables.keys().cloned().collect();};
        if program.program.native{return bound.schema.tables.keys().cloned().collect();}
        tables.extend(ir::read_sites(&program.program).into_iter().map(|site|site.table));
        tables.extend(ir::write_tables(&program.program).into_iter().map(|(table,_)|table));
        named(&serde_json::to_value(&program.program.args).unwrap(),bound,&mut tables);
    }
    loop{
        let before=tables.len();for table in tables.clone(){if let Some(definition)=bound.schema.tables.get(&table){named(&serde_json::to_value(definition).unwrap(),bound,&mut tables);}}
        for maintain in &bound.schema.maintains{if tables.contains(&maintain.source)||tables.contains(&maintain.target){tables.insert(maintain.source.clone());tables.insert(maintain.target.clone());}}
        if tables.len()==before{break;}
    }tables
}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn evidence(sources:&[(String,String)],bound: &Bound, inv: &security::Invariant, c: &Counterexample) -> serde_json::Value {
    let args = actual_args(c)
        .iter()
        .map(|(name, value)| (name, safe(value, None)))
        .collect::<BTreeMap<_, _>>();
    let old = c.old.as_ref().map(|row| image(bound, &inv.table, row));
    let next = image(bound, &inv.table, &c.row);
    let summary = format!(
        "{}({}) as {} → ok; {} {} -> {}; candidate invariant {} only {} (finite generated-fixture replay, not proof)",
        c.operation,
        serde_json::to_string(&args).unwrap(),
        c.viewer,
        inv.target_label(),
        old.as_ref()
            .map(|v| v.to_string())
            .unwrap_or_else(|| "absent".into()),
        next,
        inv.target,
        inv.predicate
    );
    // @ref LLP 3000.037.000#1-default-verdict — compact exact fields retain the full replay in JSON.
    // The default verdict shows the candidate's exact fields; full images and
    // replay history remain in JSON and the expanded review summary.
    let mut fields=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    if let Some(column)=&inv.column {fields.insert(column.clone());}
    let selected=std::cell::RefCell::new(fields);
    if let Some(policy)=&inv.policy {policy.any_term(&|term| {
        if let snapback4_core::schema::RuleTerm::Field(column)|snapback4_core::schema::RuleTerm::OldField(column)|snapback4_core::schema::RuleTerm::NextField(column)=term {selected.borrow_mut().insert(column.clone());}
        false
    });}
    let focused=|row:&serde_json::Value| row.as_object().map(|row|row.iter().filter(|(key,_)|selected.borrow().contains(*key)).map(|(k,v)|(k.clone(),v.clone())).collect::<serde_json::Map<_,_>>()).map(serde_json::Value::Object).unwrap_or(serde_json::Value::Null);
    let compact_summary=format!("{} as {} → ok; {} {} -> {} (bounded replay; not proof)",c.operation,c.viewer,inv.target_label(),old.as_ref().map(|row|focused(row).to_string()).unwrap_or_else(||"absent".into()),focused(&next));
    let history=c.history.iter().map(|step|json!({"operation":step.operation,"viewer":step.viewer,"args":step.args.iter().map(|(k,v)|(k,safe(v,None))).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>(),"state":step.result["state"]})).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let dependencies=dependencies(sources,bound,inv,c);
    let committed=c.history.last().and_then(|step|step.result.get("committedRows")).and_then(|value|serde_json::from_value::<BTreeMap<String,Vec<snapback4_core::Value>>>(value.clone()).ok());
    let supporting = c
        .supporting_rows
        .iter()
        .filter(|(table,_)|dependencies.contains(*table))
        .map(|(table, rows)| {
            (
                table,
                rows.iter()
                    .map(|r| image(bound, table, r))
                    .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            )
        })
        .collect::<BTreeMap<_, _>>();
    let mut generated_ids=std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
    let mut add_id=|value:&snapback4_core::Value|{if let snapback4_core::Value::Id(value)=value&&value.len()==26&&value.bytes().all(|b|b"0123456789ABCDEFGHJKMNPQRSTVWXYZ".contains(&b)){generated_ids.insert(value.clone());}};
    let row_ids=|table:&str,row:&snapback4_core::Value,add:&mut dyn FnMut(&snapback4_core::Value)|{if let snapback4_core::Value::Row(fields)=row{for (column,value) in fields.iter(){if column=="id"||bound.schema.table(table).and_then(|t|t.columns.get(column)).is_some_and(|k|matches!(k.required(),ColumnType::Id|ColumnType::Ref{..}|ColumnType::Principal)){add(value);}}}};
    row_ids(&inv.table,&c.row,&mut add_id);if let Some(old)=&c.old{row_ids(&inv.table,old,&mut add_id);}
    for (table,rows) in &c.supporting_rows{if dependencies.contains(table){for row in rows{row_ids(table,row,&mut add_id);}}}
    if let Some(rows)=&committed{for (table,rows) in rows{if dependencies.contains(table){for row in rows{row_ids(table,row,&mut add_id);}}}}
    for value in actual_args(c).values(){add_id(value);}for step in &c.history{for value in step.args.values(){add_id(value);}}
    let mut value=json!({"compactSummary":compact_summary,"generatedIds":generated_ids,"history":history,"supportingRows":supporting,"summary":summary,"candidate":format!("invariant {} only {}",inv.target_label(),inv.predicate),"operation":c.operation,"viewer":c.viewer,"args":args,"effect":c.effect,"old":old,"row":next,"reachability":c.reachability,"scope":c.scope});
    if let Some(rows)=committed{value["committedRows"]=json!(rows.iter().filter(|(table,_)|dependencies.contains(*table)).map(|(table,rows)|(table,rows.iter().map(|row|image(bound,table,row)).collect::<Vec<_>>())).collect::<BTreeMap<_,_>>());}
    super::super::security_reviews::witness::stamp(value)
}
mod approval_applicability;
use approval_applicability::{approval_trigger,precise_approval};
fn candidate(sources: &[(String, String)], line: &str) -> Option<security::Invariant> {
    let mut sources = sources.to_vec();
    sources.push(("$dx11-question.q".into(), format!("{line}\n")));
    let bound = snapback4_lang::compile(&sources);
    if !bound.diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    let mut inv = bound
        .invariants
        .into_iter()
        .find(|i| i.site.file == "$dx11-question.q")?;
    inv.site.file = format!("$derived/dx11/{}", hash(line.as_bytes()));
    Some(inv)
        .mutate(
            &c.operation,
            None,
            Context::new(&c.viewer, low.clone()).at(challenges::NOW),
        )
        .ok()?;
    if serde_json::to_value(&second).ok()?["state"] != "sent" {
        return None;
    }
    let row = engine
        .store()
        .rows(&inv.table)
        .ok()?
        .into_iter()
        .find(|r| r.field("id").ok() == Some(&id))?;
    if !matches!((old.field(column).ok()?,row.field(column).ok()?),(Value::Int(a),Value::Int(b)) if a>b)
    {
        return None;
    }
    let mut next = c.clone();
    next.args = low.clone();
    next.old = Some(old);
    next.row = row;
    next.history.clear();
    next.supporting_rows.clear();
    for (table, row) in initial {
        next.supporting_rows.entry(table).or_default().push(row);
    }
    next.scope="two actual operations on the reconstructed generated fixture after an observed update; fixture initialization is not claimed reachable from an empty application".into();
    if let Some(step) = c.history.last() {
        let mut first_step = step.clone();
        first_step.operation = c.operation.clone();
        first_step.viewer = c.viewer.clone();
        first_step.args = high;
        first_step.result = serde_json::to_value(first).ok()?;
        next.history.push(first_step);
        let mut second_step = step.clone();
        second_step.operation = c.operation.clone();
        second_step.viewer = c.viewer.clone();
        second_step.args = low;
        second_step.result = serde_json::to_value(second).ok()?;
        next.history.push(second_step);
    }
    Some(next)
}
/// Deployment and its prescribed acknowledgment consume the same actual
/// campaign verdict, without a different display-only counterexample chooser.
pub(in crate::verbs) fn apply_report(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound,report:&security::Report,notes:&[Diagnostic],evidence:&mut [Option<serde_json::Value>]) {
    for (index,note) in notes.iter().enumerate().filter(|(_,n)|n.code=="N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY") {
        if let Some(verdict)=report.invariants.iter().find(|v|v.invariant.site==note.site&&v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())) {
            let value=self::evidence(sources,bound,&verdict.invariant,verdict.counterexample.as_ref().unwrap());
            if super::super::security_reviews::witness::concrete(&value){evidence[index]=Some(value);}
        }
    }
}
/// Fixed work per mode; a generated presentation cache never supplies a gate.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#13-witness-backed-enforcement-and-shipping-precision — every gate replays current source even when advice is cached.
pub(in crate::verbs) fn collect(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],notes:&[Diagnostic],expanded:bool,options:&Options)->Vec<Option<serde_json::Value>> {
    let key=hash(&serde_json::to_vec(&("sec10-witness-8",sources,notes,record::capture(sources,None).ok())).unwrap());
    let path=project.schema_dir().join("generated/security-witnesses.json");
    let gate=|index:usize|super::super::security_reviews::gated(&notes[index].code)&&precise_approval(bound,&notes[index]);
    let cached=std::fs::read(&path).ok().and_then(|b|serde_json::from_slice::<serde_json::Value>(&b).ok()).filter(|v|v["key"]==key).and_then(|v|serde_json::from_value::<Vec<Option<serde_json::Value>>>(v["values"].clone()).ok()).filter(|v|v.len()==notes.len());
    let mut result=vec![None;notes.len()];
    if let Some(cached)=&cached{for (index,value) in cached.iter().enumerate(){if !gate(index)&&notes[index].code!="N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY"{result[index]=value.clone();}}}
    let selected=candidates(bound,sources,notes);
    for enforcement in [true,false] {
        if !enforcement&&(!expanded||cached.is_some()){continue;}
        let selected=selected.iter().filter(|(index,_)|gate(*index)==enforcement).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        if selected.is_empty(){continue;}
        let mut questions=bound.clone();questions.invariants=selected.iter().map(|(_,inv)|inv.clone()).collect();
        let mut campaign=challenges::Campaign::work_bounded(if enforcement{3072}else{8192});
        challenges::generated_campaign(&questions,&mut campaign,false);
        for (index,inv) in selected {
            if result[*index].is_some()&&notes[*index].code!="N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT"{continue;}
            if let Some(c)=campaign.violations.iter().filter(|c|c.invariant_site==inv.site.to_string()&&c.reachability.is_reachable()).max_by_key(|c| {
                let decrease=inv.column.as_ref().and_then(|column|Some((c.old.as_ref()?.field(column).ok()?,c.row.field(column).ok()?))).is_some_and(|(old,next)|matches!((old,next),(snapback4_core::Value::Int(a),snapback4_core::Value::Int(b)) if a>b));
                (decrease,c.history.len())
            }) {let replay=decreasing_edit(bound,inv,c);let value=evidence(sources,bound,inv,replay.as_ref().unwrap_or(c));if super::super::security_reviews::witness::concrete(&value){
                let decrease=|v:&serde_json::Value|v["old"].as_object().is_some_and(|fields|fields.iter().any(|(field,old)|old.as_i64().zip(v["row"][field].as_i64()).is_some_and(|(a,b)|a>b)));
                if result[*index].is_none()||decrease(&value)&&!result[*index].as_ref().is_some_and(decrease){result[*index]=Some(value);}
            }}
        }
    }
    if expanded&&notes.iter().any(|n|n.code=="N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY"&&!n.site.file.contains("/label-consistency/")) {
        let mut deploy=options.clone();deploy.flags.insert("security-deploy".into(),String::new());
        let report=super::report(project,bound,sources,&deploy);apply_report(sources,bound,&report,notes,&mut result);
    }
    // Reapply current eligibility even to cached advice: editable presentation
    // evidence cannot promote an unresolved approval question into a gate.
    for (index,value) in result.iter_mut().enumerate(){if !precise_approval(bound,&notes[index]){if let Some(value)=value{value["advisory"]=true.into();}}}
    if expanded {let advice=result.iter().enumerate().map(|(index,value)|if gate(index)||notes[index].code=="N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY"{None}else{value.clone()}).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let temporary=path.with_extension(format!("{}.tmp",std::process::id()));
        if std::fs::write(&temporary,serde_json::to_vec(&json!({"key":key,"values":advice})).unwrap()).is_ok(){let _=std::fs::rename(&temporary,&path);}let _=std::fs::remove_file(temporary);
    }
    result
}

        let note=Diagnostic::new(&inv.site,"N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY","participants: committed conservation","refund before removal");let notes=[note];let evidence1=[Some(first.clone())];
        let initial=crate::verbs::security_reviews::review_evidence(&project,&sources,&notes,None,&evidence1).unwrap();

        crate::verbs::security_reviews::review_evidence(&project,&sources,&notes,Some((&initial[0].id,&reason)),&evidence1).unwrap();

        assert_ne!(initial[0].id,next[0].id);assert!(!next[0].acknowledged);assert!(next[0].reason.is_none());
    }

//! Conjunctive payload write authority shared by all write arms.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#2-column-write-authority — defaults and both upsert arms use the ordinary rule boundary.
use super::*;
impl Table {
    /// Literal defaults are initialization, whether omitted or explicitly supplied.
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#14-wave-30-default-initialization-dx13 — equality is limited to the insert arm.
    pub fn column_initialization(&self, column: &str) -> RuleExpr {
        let field = RuleTerm::Field(column.into());
        let mut alternatives = vec![RuleExpr::Eq(field.clone(), RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null))];
        if let Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(value)) = self.defaults.get(column) {
            alternatives.push(RuleExpr::Eq(field, RuleTerm::Literal(value.clone())));
        }
        RuleExpr::Or(alternatives)
    }
    pub fn guarded_rule(&self, effect: Effect) -> std::borrow::Cow<'_, RuleExpr> {
        let base = if effect == Effect::Update { self.update_rule_judged() } else { std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(self.rules.for_effect(effect)) };
        if self.column_writes.is_empty() || !matches!(effect,Effect::Insert|Effect::Update) { return base; }
        let mut rules=vec![base.into_owned()];
        for (column,policy) in &self.column_writes {
            let same = if effect == Effect::Insert { self.column_initialization(column) }
                else { RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::OldField(column.clone()),RuleTerm::Field(column.clone())) };
            rules.push(RuleExpr::Or(vec![same,if effect==Effect::Insert {policy.clone()} else {policy.old_and_next()}]));
        }
        std::borrow::Cow::Owned(RuleExpr::And(rules))
    }
}
pub fn validate(schema:&Schema)->Vec<SchemaIssue>{
    let mut issues=Vec::new();
    for (name,t) in &schema.tables {
        for (column,rule) in t.column_writes.iter().chain(&t.frozen_columns) {
            let path=format!("tables.{name}.column_writes.{column}");
            if column=="id" || !t.columns.contains_key(column) || t.engine_field(column) || t.maintained.contains_key(column) || t.sequences.contains_key(column) || schema.maintain_for(name).is_some() || schema.timelines.iter().any(|timeline|timeline.owns(name)||timeline.maps(name)) || schema.receipts.iter().any(|r|r.messages==*name||r.members==*name||r.runs==*name) || t.written_by.contains_key(column) || t.on_delete.get(column)==Some(&references::OnDelete::SetNull) {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue{path:path.clone(),message:"column write/freeze needs an ordinary payload column without owner-field bypass or on delete set null".into()});
            }
            super::validate_rule(schema,t,rule,false,false,&path,&mut issues);
            if rule.any_term(&|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::OldField(_)|RuleTerm::NextField(_))) {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue{path,message:"column write/freeze policies use current-row fields, never named transition images".into()});
            }
        }
    }
    issues
}

## Summary

Snapback 4 is one of two parallel candidates for the successor to Snapback 2,
and the sibling of Snapback 3, not its successor. It builds
[LLP 2000.000](./2000.000-keep-the-core-flip-two-substrates.rfc.md) as
written: the Snapback 2 core kept verbatim, two substrates flipped up front —
resources metered at runtime instead of priced statically, and a client that
replicates the viewer's declared partition instead of caching receipted
results — proved first on the hub fixture, with a deterministic simulator as
the primary harness. Snapback 3 ([LLP 2000](./2000-snapback3.rfc.md), led by
Codex / GPT-6 Astra) keeps the same two questions open and discovers their
answers in its first slice. Snapback 2 ([LLP 1000](./1000-snapback2.explainer.md))
continues as the incumbent and the control. All three are judged by one
pre-registered comparison on one clock.

This document records the author's decisions of 2026-09-12 that created the
track, restates the bet on one page so a reader can orient without the brief,
fixes the bakeoff protocol, draws the fence, and plans the first four weeks.
The design argument and its evidence stay in LLP 2000.000. Only running code
will bind (`rules/RULES.md`).

## 1. Decisions (author, 2026-09-12)

On 2026-09-12 Charlie Cheever asked whether Claude Fable 5.1 should lead a
Snapback 4 while Codex / GPT-6 Astra leads Snapback 3 and Snapback 2
continues, "and see which one turns out best." The lead answered yes with
conditions; the author accepted them ("Sounds good") and asked for this
document. The decisions below are therefore the lead's proposal as accepted
by the author. Where the author has not separately ruled, the text says so.

1. **Three tracks, one comparison.** Snapback 2 continues as the incumbent
   and the control. Snapback 3 builds LLP 2000; Snapback 4 builds LLP
   2000.000. This is a bakeoff of *bets* — discover the substrates in the
   slice versus decide them first — not of models. The result will say more
   about the bets than about the leads, and §3 is written so that it can.
2. **The name is Snapback 4.** Siblings, not a sequence: "4" does not mean
   "after 3." A separate number gives the track its own corpus root,
   directory, package identity and data directory with no rename when the
   bakeoff resolves, and files never move in LLP. If Snapback 4 loses, it is
   a number that never shipped. "Snapback 3b" was considered and rejected:
   it reads as a variant of 3, and this is a different bet.
3. **Snapback 4 builds LLP 2000.000 as written.** Its §14 open questions are
   the lead's to decide inside the track, recorded in §7 as they are
   decided. The author rules on the bar, the comparison and promotion (§3).
4. **One owner on the core.** The lead writes the engine, the store and the
   client contract. Codex lanes build the fixture generator, the reference
   applications, the device witnesses and the periphery. Blind reviews from
   two model families are reserved for the two disclosure-sensitive
   mechanisms: partition inversion of read rules (LLP 2000.000 §7.1) and
   the meter's cut behaviour on non-uniform scans (LLP 2000.000 §6). This
   departs from the standing rule that Codex codes and Claude orchestrates,
   deliberately, for the core only.
5. **The clock is four weeks** from the first commit under `snapback4/` to
   the first slice running on the hub fixture. At the clock the author kills
   the track, merges its substrates into a survivor, or continues it. There
   is no third month without a ruling.
6. **Shared and not shared.** Shared with Snapback 3: the LLP corpus, the
   Clique harness and scorers, the fixtures, the briefs, and the simulator
   once either track has one. Not shared: code, except the Snapback 2 pieces
   both are expected to copy (LLP 2000.000 §5.1).
7. **Snapback 2 keeps shipping and freezes new substrate work for the
   duration.** Fix-forward, the loop bar, acquisition and papercuts continue;
   no new certificate axes, client custody mechanisms or ceiling changes.
   This is accepted here as a condition of the bakeoff. Making it binding on
   concurrent Snapback 2 sessions needs one line in `rules/NOT-DOING.md`'s
   Moves ledger, which this document does not write.
8. **Reading load.** This root, updated in place, and one append-only
   journal, `snapback4/JOURNAL.md`. Sub-LLPs only for a decision that must
   outlive the journal. No panels beyond decision 4's two reviews.

## 2. The bet on one page

**Keep verbatim from Snapback 2** (LLP 2000.000 §5.1): the rule language
with its uniformity and hoist analysis, `decided_true` and the
structural-fact refusals; one data language, Quarry, with `--` comments,
`require … else CODE`, `shape`, `merge`, `walk`, `chunk`; the value model
and its conformance corpus; typed read and write states with no silent state
and the refusal envelope `{code, family, site, message, rewrite, guide}`; the
successor classifier (widening additive, checked narrowing, exact projection
ownership); `deliver`'s engine-computed audience, tombstones and
`bootstrap`; `schedule`, `job`, `chunk` and rehearsal; personas; `init`,
`dev`, `check`, `doctor`, `run`, `test`, `why`, `trace`, `guide`; H0
`deploy`, `rollback`, `secret`; channels; assets; the word-prefix index.

# LLP 2000.000: Snapback 3 — keep the core, flip two substrates, start at hub scale

**Type:** RFC
**Status:** Draft
**Role:** Second opinion on LLP 2000; since 2026-09-12 also the design brief Snapback 4 builds (LLP 3000)
**Systems:** Snapback 3 product architecture, bounded computation, client data model, client API vocabulary, language and tooling, effects runtime, verification (simulation), performance budgets, process
**Author:** Claude Fable 5.1, at Charlie Cheever's request
**Date:** 2026-09-12
**Revised:** 2026-09-12 (adopted as the Snapback 4 design brief by LLP 3000 decision 3); 2026-09-12 (initial)
**Related:** [LLP 2000](./2000-snapback3.rfc.md) (parent), [LLP 3000](./3000-snapback4.explainer.md) (the Snapback 4 root that builds this brief), LLP 0000, [LLP 0329](./0329-agent-native-rebuild-diagnosis.research.md), [LLP 0329.014.004](./0329.014.004-bounded-data-programs-v1-design.rfc.md), [LLP 0329.016](./0329.016-agentic-dev-loop-from-scratch.rfc.md), [LLP 1000](./1000-snapback2.explainer.md), [LLP 1001](./1001-query-engine.spec.md), [LLP 1002](./1002-dev-loop.spec.md), [LLP 1012](./1012-local-first-v1.spec.md), [LLP 1018.003](./1018.003-truthful-and-composed-client.decision.md), [LLP 1018.008](./1018.008-elegance-after-the-corrections.research.md), [LLP 1018.008.007](./1018.008.007-affordable-offline-acquisition.research.md), [LLP 1018.008.008](./1018.008.008-locality-of-application-changes.research.md), [LLP 1018.008.009](./1018.008.009-residual-ergonomic-gaps.research.md), [LLP 1019](./1019-derived-delivery-custody.rfc.md), [LLP 1020](./1020-day-two-evolution-and-legible-ceilings.rfc.md), [LLP 1023](./1023-clique-builder-jump.rfc.md), [LLP 1024](./1024-single-lane-delivered-search.rfc.md), [LLP 1025](./1025-digest-final-emission-at-hub-scale.research.md), `rules/RULES.md`, `rules/NOT-DOING.md`, `benchmarks/runs/2026-09-05-clique-full-3x3/holistic-scores.md`

## 1. Summary

LLP 2000 is right that the whole application is the unit of design, right
that ownership comes before abstraction, and right to ask for deterministic
simulation and an early real deployment. Its weakness is that it treats the
two structural choices that produced Snapback 2's costs as open research
questions, and then proposes a first experiment at exactly the scale where
those costs are invisible. This document takes a position on both, names
what to keep from Snapback 2 verbatim, and rewrites the first slice so it
can fail for the right reasons.

The position, in one paragraph. Snapback 2's *core* is excellent and should
be copied: the rule language and its uniformity analysis, the structural-fact
family, one data language, typed states with no silence, refusals that paste
a rewrite, personas, the successor classifier, the derived per-recipient
log, the loop verbs and their sub-second budgets. Two *substrates* under
that core are where the record shows the hours went, and both should be
flipped rather than re-investigated: (1) the static certificate should keep
certifying **authorization and shape** and stop pricing **bytes and steps**,
which move to a deterministic runtime meter with honest `capped`/abort
outcomes; and (2) the client should hold **a replica of the viewer's declared
partition** — the local-first shape every shipped sync engine and Snapback
2's own `deliver` log converge on — instead of a per-query result cache
proven by receipts. Those two flips dissolve most of the machinery that
leaked into the application (receipts, coverage, halos, validators, grants,
ID slices, drains, `withHistory`, follower inboxes, per-table retention
words) and turn the 28-second acquisition into a bulk stream whose floor is
the device's write speed. The first slice should be built on the **hub
fixture** (1,000 memberships, 500 follows, 100,000 messages), with the
day-two change designed before the day-one screen, a native client in the
first slice, and a deterministic simulator as the primary test harness.

Three claims this document makes that LLP 2000 does not:

1. **The costs have two named causes, and both are decisions, not
   mysteries.** Byte-priced static certification and the receipt-proven
   result cache account for every ergonomic residual in LLP 1018.008.009
   except documentation. §2 shows the mapping.
2. **The first experiment must include the hub.** Snapback 2 was "elegant on
   day one and hostile on day two" (LLP 1020) and broke at 1,000 memberships
   (LLPs 1023, 1024, 1025). A two-principal slice will pass and teach nothing.
3. **The process is part of the product.** Seventy-one LLPs, over two
   thousand commits, and seven ceiling raises in fourteen days produced a
   sound system nobody can hold in their head. Snapback 3 needs one owner on
# Rules

**Snapback 2 optimizes the edit-to-verified loop — the agent's building an app on it, and ours building it.**

Speed is the constraint. Everything below exists to keep the edit-to-verified loop short
and to stop this repository accreting apparatus faster than anyone can remove it. These
rules bind `snapback2/` and the LLPs numbered 1000 and up; Snapback 1 is baselined, not
exempt, and its numbers may only fall.

Each rule is **[check]** (enforced by `caps`) or **[review]** (enforced by a human).

## Budgets — every budget is a trade, not a limit

- **5 blocking checks, 60s total.** A sixth requires deleting one, in the same PR. **[check]**
- **59 documents in the working set** (`llp/current/`). Today's count. A 60th requires
  archiving one — which is just removing the link. Snapback 2 aims to leave this at 15;
  Snapback 1's links are archived at promotion. **[check]**
- **12 documents in the foundation** (`llp/foundation/`). **[check]**
- **This file: 700 words.** If it grows, something becomes a check or stops being a rule. **[check]**
- **1,500 lines per source file, baseline 204.** Generated files exempt — and generated
  files are built, not committed; so are benchmark run records under `benchmarks/runs/`,
  which are evidence, not source. The baseline is Snapback 1's oversize count on the day
  the rule landed; it may not grow, and `snapback2/` contributes zero to it. **[check]**

## Blocking checks

`caps` · `ref-check` · `snapback2/scripts/test.sh` · `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs --check` · `check-inventories`

## Loop shape

- **Every check reports all failures in one run.** Fail-fast is banned. **[check]**
- **Nothing blocks on anything slower than 60 seconds.** Slow verification runs on a
  schedule, attributed to the breaking commit, and files a bounded set of issues. **[review]**
- **Fix loops get 3 rounds.** Then stop: escalate to a human or descope. **[review]**

## Time budgets

Tracked every run of `snapback2/scripts/measure.mjs` (appends `snapback2/metrics/loop.jsonl`).
A regression is a P0 with a name on it.

| | budget |
|---|---|
| Incremental rebuild after a one-line edit | 15s |
| Test what you just changed | 30s |
| Blocking gate suite | 60s |
| Full build, warm cache | 5 min |
| **`snapback2 check` on a program (the agent's product latency)** | **1s** |
| **`snapback2 doctor` boot-to-ready** | **0.5s** |

## Scope

- **`NOT-DOING.md` is binding.** Moving something onto the doing-list means writing why
  and taking something off. **[review]**
- **Delete; don't deprecate.** No compat shims or migration paths before promotion. **[review]**
- **Nothing is compiled, transpiled, or generated at runtime that could be built ahead of
  time.** **[review]**
- **A spec needs an implementer and a date, or it isn't written.** **[review]**
- **Only implemented specs bind.** A document with no running code behind it is a
  proposal, and a proposal cannot block a PR or justify a check. **[review]**

## Agents

- **Agents remove apparatus freely and add none.** An agent PR that adds a check, script,
  registry, config file, or design doc needs explicit human approval saying so. **[review]**
- **2026-09-06 session exception:** the user delegated the search implementation
  decisions; one `snapback2-core/build.rs` may generate pinned Unicode tables at
  build time, alongside the existing package build. No new gate, registry,
  runtime generator, or Unicode dependency is authorized by this exception. **[review]**
- **Snapback 2 LLPs are numbered 1000 and up**; `llp-create` must be told. **[review]**
- **Snapback 4 LLPs are numbered 3000 and up** (`snapback4/`, LLP 3000); it adopts these budgets
  and loop shape as its own and keeps its scope in `snapback4/NOT-DOING.md`. **[review]**

## Shared machines

- Warm, named lanes, one per stream of work. Never clone fresh; reuse the worktree.
- **Never `git stash`.** Use a temporary commit.
- Kill only PIDs you recorded at launch. Never by name or pattern.
