//! Conjunctive payload write authority shared by all write arms.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#2-column-write-authority — defaults and both upsert arms use the ordinary rule boundary.
use super::*;
impl Table {
    /// Literal defaults are initialization, whether omitted or explicitly supplied.
    // @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#14-wave-30-default-initialization-dx13 — equality is limited to the insert arm.
    pub fn column_initialization(&self, column: &str) -> RuleExpr {
        let field = RuleTerm::Field(column.into());
        let mut alternatives = vec![RuleExpr::Eq(field.clone(), RuleTerm::Literal(Value::Null))];
        if let Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(value)) = self.defaults.get(column) {
            alternatives.push(RuleExpr::Eq(field, RuleTerm::Literal(value.clone())));
        }
        RuleExpr::Or(alternatives)
    }
    pub fn guarded_rule(&self, effect: Effect) -> std::borrow::Cow<'_, RuleExpr> {
        let base = if effect == Effect::Update { self.update_rule_judged() } else { std::borrow::Cow::Borrowed(self.rules.for_effect(effect)) };
        if self.column_writes.is_empty() || !matches!(effect,Effect::Insert|Effect::Update) { return base; }
        let mut rules=vec![base.into_owned()];
        for (column,policy) in &self.column_writes {
            let same = if effect == Effect::Insert { self.column_initialization(column) }
                else { RuleExpr::Eq(RuleTerm::OldField(column.clone()),RuleTerm::Field(column.clone())) };
            rules.push(RuleExpr::Or(vec![same,if effect==Effect::Insert {policy.clone()} else {policy.old_and_next()}]));
        }
        std::borrow::Cow::Owned(RuleExpr::And(rules))
    }
}
pub fn validate(schema:&Schema)->Vec<SchemaIssue>{
    let mut issues=Vec::new();
    for (name,t) in &schema.tables {
        for (column,rule) in t.column_writes.iter().chain(&t.frozen_columns) {
            let path=format!("tables.{name}.column_writes.{column}");
            if column=="id" || !t.columns.contains_key(column) || t.engine_field(column) || t.maintained.contains_key(column) || t.sequences.contains_key(column) || schema.maintain_for(name).is_some() || schema.timelines.iter().any(|timeline|timeline.owns(name)||timeline.maps(name)) || schema.receipts.iter().any(|r|r.messages==*name||r.members==*name||r.runs==*name) || t.written_by.contains_key(column) || t.on_delete.get(column)==Some(&references::OnDelete::SetNull) {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue{path:path.clone(),message:"column write/freeze needs an ordinary payload column without owner-field bypass or on delete set null".into()});
            }
            super::validate_rule(schema,t,rule,false,false,&path,&mut issues);
            if rule.any_term(&|term|matches!(term,RuleTerm::OldField(_)|RuleTerm::NextField(_))) {
                issues.push(SchemaIssue{path,message:"column write/freeze policies use current-row fields, never named transition images".into()});
            }
        }
    }
    issues
}
    labels::invariants(&docs,&catalog,bound,&mut out);
    out.extend(conservation::contracts(&docs,&catalog,bound).into_iter().map(|(inv,_)|inv));
    out
}
pub fn notes(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Vec<Diagnostic>{
    invariants(sources,bound).into_iter().map(|i|{
        let transition=transition(&i).is_some();
        let workflow=["/sibling-approval/","/approval-history/","/repeat-create/","/parent-state/","/child-stage/"].iter().any(|label|i.site.file.contains(label));
        let message=if transition{format!("{}: inferred effect prerequisite {}; judge before entering the selected destination, permitting atomic approval consumption; a replayed violation needs a specification decision",i.target_label(),i.predicate)}else{format!("{}: derived candidate {} only {}; a replayed violation needs a specification decision",i.target_label(),i.target,i.predicate)};
        let repair=if advisory(&i)&&!i.site.file.contains("/label-consistency/"){"initial and edit audiences may deliberately differ; this projected guard is advice only; declare independent creation/update intent if the specification requires it, then run test"}else if i.site.file.contains("/participant-conservation/"){"refund the matching participant charge in the removal transaction, refuse removal while a charge remains, or record this deliberate no-refund removal with check --ack-security ID --reason '<spec rationale>'"}else if i.site.file.contains("/label-consistency/"){"a backfill only initializes existing rows; if the formula is the ongoing meaning, declare invariant T always c = f(x); otherwise retain the intentional setter (advice only)"}else if workflow{"require the named approval/parent/child prerequisite before this governed effect, consume one-use guards atomically, or record this individual alternative path with check --ack-security ID --reason '<spec rationale>'"}else{"enforce column write/freeze authority, declare matching update-only creation intent, or record this individual rule's deliberate design with check --ack-security ID --reason '<spec rationale>'"};
        Diagnostic::new(&i.site,"N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY",message,repair).guide("security")
    }).collect()
}
/// Semantic witness inputs include custom committed-state contracts whose
/// dependency tables are not represented by a policy/syntax expression.
pub fn witness_dependencies(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant)->BTreeSet<String>{
    let mut tables=inv.dependencies();
    if inv.site.file.contains("/participant-conservation/"){
        let cached=bound.security_conservation.get(&inv.site.to_string()).cloned();
        let contract=cached.or_else(||{
            let docs=sources.iter().map(|(file,text)|crate::parse_document(file,text).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            let catalog=Catalog::build(docs.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
            conservation::contracts(&docs,&catalog,bound).into_iter().find(|(candidate,_)|candidate.site==inv.site).map(|(_,contract)|contract)
        });
        if let Some(contract)=contract{tables.insert(contract.ledger);tables.extend(contract.refund_ledger);}else{
            // A missing custom judgment cannot silently become dependency-free.
            tables.extend(bound.schema.tables.keys().cloned());
        }
    }tables
}
pub fn augmented(sources:&[(String,String)],bound:&Bound)->Bound{
    let mut out=bound.clone();let mut inputs=BTreeMap::new();let mut reissues=BTreeMap::new();let mut uncertain=BTreeMap::new();out.invariants.extend(collect(sources,bound,&mut inputs,&mut reissues,&mut uncertain).into_iter().filter(|i|!advisory(i)));out.security_approval_checks=approval_checks::collect(sources,bound);out.security_approval_reissues.extend(reissues);out.security_approval_uncertain_writes.extend(uncertain);for (stamp,columns) in inputs{out.security_approval_inputs.entry(stamp).or_default().extend(columns);}
    let docs=sources.iter().map(|(f,s)|crate::parse_document(f,s).0).collect::<Vec<_>>();let catalog=Catalog::build(docs.clone(),&mut Vec::new());
    out.security_conservation.extend(conservation::contracts(&docs,&catalog,bound).into_iter().map(|(inv,contract)|(inv.site.to_string(),contract)));
    // Declared write audiences are runtime contracts, not inferred spec intent.
    // Their replay failures cannot be acknowledged as deliberate asymmetry.
    let mut contracts=Vec::new();
    for (table,t) in bound.schema.visible_tables(){for (column,policy) in &t.column_writes{
        let site=Site::new("column-write-contract",1,1);
        add(&mut contracts,table,column,"insert",RuleExpr::Or(vec![t.column_initialization(column),policy.clone()]),&site);
        add(&mut contracts,table,column,"update",policy.clone(),&site);
    }}
    for contract in &mut contracts{contract.site.file=contract.site.file.replacen("$derived/","$enforced/",1);}
    out.invariants.extend(contracts);out
}

//! Positive activation on the exact approved row bounds individual gates.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#13-witness-backed-enforcement-and-shipping-precision — unknown applicability is advisory.
use super::*;
// A coupled activation flag must be required on the very row whose approval
// is recorded. An unmatched/unknown applicability remains an advisory guess.
pub(super) fn approval_trigger(bound:&Bound,table:&str,column:&str)->Option<String>{
    use snapback4_core::ir::{self,Expr as E,Stmt as S};
    use snapback4_core::Value as V;
    let t=bound.schema.table(table)?;let stem=column.to_lowercase().split("approv").next()?.to_owned();
    if stem.is_empty(){return None;}
    fn id_key<'a>(mut value:&'a E,vars:&'a BTreeMap<String,E>,arg:&str)->bool{
        for _ in 0..32{let E::Variable(name)=value else{return false;};if name==arg{return true;}let Some(next)=vars.get(name)else{return false;};value=next;}false
    }
    fn key(read:&ir::Read,vars:&BTreeMap<String,E>,table:&str,arg:&str)->bool{read.table==table&&read.index=="byId"&&read.key.len()==1&&id_key(&read.key[0],vars,arg)}
    fn row(e:&E,vars:&BTreeMap<String,E>,table:&str,arg:&str,depth:usize)->bool{
        if depth>32{return false;}
        match e{
            E::Get(read)=>key(read,vars,table,arg),
            E::Variable(name)=>vars.get(name).is_some_and(|e|row(e,vars,table,arg,depth+1)),
            E::Block{body,value}=>{let mut vars=vars.clone();for stmt in body{match stmt{S::Const{name,value}|S::Let{name,value}=>{vars.insert(name.clone(),value.clone());},S::Require{..}=>{},_=>return false,}}row(value,&vars,table,arg,depth+1)},
            _=>false,
        }
    }
    fn positive(e:&E,vars:&BTreeMap<String,E>,table:&str,arg:&str,column:&str,depth:usize)->bool{
        if depth>32{return false;}
        let field=|e:&E|matches!(e,E::Field{base,name} if name==column&&row(base,vars,table,arg,0));
        match e{
            E::Field{..}=>field(e),
            E::Variable(name)=>vars.get(name).is_some_and(|e|positive(e,vars,table,arg,column,depth+1)),
            E::Binary{op:ir::BinaryOp::And,left,right}=>positive(left,vars,table,arg,column,depth+1)||positive(right,vars,table,arg,column,depth+1),
            E::Binary{op:ir::BinaryOp::Or,left,right}=>positive(left,vars,table,arg,column,depth+1)&&positive(right,vars,table,arg,column,depth+1),
            E::Binary{op,left,right}=>[(left.as_ref(),right.as_ref()),(right.as_ref(),left.as_ref())].into_iter().any(|(a,b)|field(a)&&matches!((op,b),(ir::BinaryOp::Eq,E::Literal(V::Bool(true)))|(ir::BinaryOp::Ne,E::Literal(V::Bool(false))))),
            _=>false,
        }
    }
    for (trigger,kind) in &t.columns{
        if trigger==column||!trigger.to_lowercase().starts_with(&stem)||!matches!(kind.required(),ColumnType::Bool){continue;}
        'producer:for p in &bound.programs{
            let args=p.program.args.iter().filter_map(|(name,kind)|matches!(kind,ir::ArgType::Ref{table:parent} if parent==table).then_some(name)).collect::<Vec<_>>();
            if args.len()!=1{continue;}let arg=args[0];let mut vars=BTreeMap::new();let mut guarded=false;
            for stmt in &p.program.body{
                let write=match stmt{
                    S::Const{name,value}|S::Let{name,value}=>{vars.insert(name.clone(),value.clone());None},
                    S::Require{condition,..}=>{guarded|=positive(condition,&vars,table,arg,trigger,0);None},
                    S::WriteTarget{target,patch,..} if key(target,&vars,table,arg)=>Some(patch),
                    S::Update{table:target,key,patch} if target==table&&id_key(key,&vars,arg)=>Some(patch),
                    S::If{..}|S::For{..}|S::Assign{..}=>continue 'producer,
                    _=>None,
                };
                if guarded&&write.is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,E::Object(fields) if fields.get(column).is_some_and(|e|matches!(e,E::Literal(V::Bool(true))|E::Input(ir::Input::Now))))){return Some(trigger.clone());}
            }
        }
    }None
}
pub(super) fn precise_approval(bound:&Bound,note:&Diagnostic)->bool{
    note.code!="N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED"||note.message.split_once(':').and_then(|(subject,_)|subject.split_once('.')).is_some_and(|(table,column)|approval_trigger(bound,table,column).is_some())
}

pub fn audit(options:&Options)->i32{
    let project=project_or_exit();
    let sources=match project.sources(){Ok(s)=>s,Err(e)=>{eprintln!("audit: {e}");return 2;}};
    let bound=compiled_or_exit(snapback4_lang::compile(&sources),options.json);
    // Audit is a permission map plus bounded evidence; `test` owns full campaigns.
    let report=report(&project,&bound,&sources,options);
    let dispositions=match super::security_reviews::review(&project,&sources,&report.notes,None){Ok(d)=>d,Err(error)=>{eprintln!("audit: E_SECURITY_NOTES: {error}");return 2;}};
    if options.json {let mut value=serde_json::to_value(&report).unwrap();value["securityReviews"]=serde_json::to_value(&dispositions).unwrap();println!("{value}");}
    else {let mut human=report.clone();human.notes.retain(|n|crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review);print!("{}",human.text_with_all(options.flags.contains_key("all")));super::security_reviews::print(&dispositions,&report.notes);}
    if report.refuted(){1}else{0}
}
pub(super) fn exposure(report:&Report){
    let broad=report.exposures.iter().filter(|e|e.effects.get("read").is_some_and(|r|r=="allow"||r.starts_with("public"))).count();
    eprintln!("security exposure: {} tables, {} conditional audiences, {broad} broadly readable tables; run snapback4 audit for exact role/column predicates",report.exposures.len(),report.roles.len());
}
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#18-wave-26-security-decisions-dx9 — report enforcement facts without claiming complete security.
pub(super) fn enforced(report:&Report,bound:&Bound){println!("{}",enforced_text(report,bound));}
pub(in crate::verbs) fn enforced_text(report:&Report,bound:&Bound)->String {
    let roles=report.notes.iter().filter(|n|n.code=="N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION").count();
    let columns=bound.schema.visible_tables().map(|(_,t)|t.column_reads.len()).sum::<usize>();
    let uploads=bound.schema.visible_tables().flat_map(|(_,t)|t.columns.values()).filter(|t|t.contains_asset()).count();
    format!("check: enforced by the language — {uploads} typed upload columns with owner-bound placement; {columns} column audiences; {roles} possible self-assignable role paths detected (bounded analysis)")
}
pub(super) fn summary(report:&Report,verbose:bool){
    let proved=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Proved).count();
    let failures=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|c.reachability.is_reachable())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
    let refuted=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted).count();
    let rule_enforced=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Unproved&&v.direct_rule==security::Status::Proved).count();
    eprintln!("security: {proved} proved, {refuted} refuted; {rule_enforced} enforced by rule (additional paths not proved); {} other unproved ({} failing)",report.invariants.len()-proved-refuted-rule_enforced,failures.len());
    for v in &failures {
        let c=v.counterexample.as_ref().unwrap();
        eprintln!("  FIX {}: {} via {} as {} ({}); tighten enforcement to the declared intent, then run snapback4 test",v.invariant.site,v.invariant.target_label(),c.operation,c.viewer,serde_json::to_value(&c.reachability).unwrap().as_str().unwrap());
    }
    let supplemental=report.invariants.iter().filter(|v|v.status==security::Status::Refuted&&v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c|!c.reachability.is_reachable())).count();
    if supplemental>0 {eprintln!("  {supplemental} seed/synthetic observations (non-failing)");}
    if verbose {let mut human=report.clone();human.notes.retain(|n|crate::note_rank::tier(&n.code)!=crate::note_rank::Tier::Review);eprint!("{}",human.text_with_all(true));}
    else if !report.invariants.is_empty()||!report.campaign_coverage.is_empty(){eprintln!("  next: snapback4 test; {} coverage gaps (check --verbose/JSON keeps every reason)",report.campaign_coverage.len());}
    if !report.exposures.is_empty(){eprintln!("  review audiences and {} uncovered exposures: snapback4 audit",report.uncovered.len());}
}

/// A rule-changing preflight demands a current campaign, without pretending
/// finite tests or undeclared intent certify a deployment.
pub(super) fn release_loop(project:&Project,bound:&Bound,sources:&[(String,String)],prior:Option<&Bound>,options:&Options)->Result<Option<String>,String>{
    let changed=match prior {
        None=>true,
        Some(old)=>serde_json::to_vec(&old.schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.schema).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
            ||serde_json::to_vec(&old.invariants).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.invariants).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?
            ||serde_json::to_vec(&old.programs.iter().map(|p|&p.program).collect::<Vec<_>>()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?!=serde_json::to_vec(&bound.programs.iter().map(|p|&p.program).collect::<Vec<_>>()).map_err(|e|e.to_string())?,
//! Empty approval initialization is not an authored column authority event.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#9-integration-24-wave-26-precision — insert initialization and actual changed edits are separate boundaries.
use super::*;
use snapback4_core::{Value,schema::ColumnDefault};

// @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#14-wave-30-default-initialization-dx13 — authored write intent and runtime grants share literal initialization.
fn declared_default(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,row:&Value)->bool {
    inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|column|matches!(
        bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].defaults.get(column),
        Some(ColumnDefault::Literal(value)) if row.field(column).ok()==Some(value)))
}
pub(super) fn empty_creation(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,old:Option<&Value>,row:&Value)->bool {
    inv.target=="write" && old.is_none() && inv.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|column|
        matches!(row.field(column),Ok(Value::Null)) || declared_default(bound,inv,row))
}
pub(super) fn policy(bound:&Bound,inv:&Invariant,effect:&str)->Option<RuleExpr> {
    let policy=inv.policy.clone()?;
    if effect!="insert"||inv.target!="write" {return Some(policy);}
    let Some(column)=&inv.column else{return Some(policy);};
    Some(RuleExpr::Or(vec![bound.schema.tables[&inv.table].column_initialization(column),policy]))
}

#[cfg(test)]mod tests {
 use super::*;
 #[test]fn clearing_or_initializing_a_sibling_does_not_exempt_an_edit(){
  let b=crate::compile(&[("schema.q".into(),"table docs:\n  approved: bool default false\n  reason: text ?\n  read <- allow\n  insert <- allow\n  online only\ninvariant docs.approved write only deny\ninvariant docs.reason write only deny\n".into())]);
  assert!(b.diagnostics.is_empty(),"{:?}",b.diagnostics);
  let row=Value::Row(std::collections::BTreeMap::from([("approved".into(),Value::Bool(false)),("reason".into(),Value::Null)]).into());
  for inv in &b.invariants {assert!(empty_creation(&b,inv,None,&row));assert!(!empty_creation(&b,inv,Some(&row),&row));assert_eq!(policy(&b,inv,"update"),inv.policy);}
 }

// Only the authored literal at this exact insert site receives this exemption.
// Caller values equal to the default and later edits still require authority.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004.000#11-wave-27-precision-and-workflow-conservation — default initialization has source custody.
pub(super) fn capture(catalog:&Catalog,bound:&mut Bound){
 fn authority_fields(bound:&Bound,table:&str)->BTreeSet<String>{
  let mut out=privilege::role_columns(bound,table);
  if let Some(t)=bound.schema.tables.get(table)&&t.indexes.values().any(|index|index.unique&&index.columns().iter().any(|column|matches!(t.columns.get(column).map(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::required),Some(snapback4_core::schema::ColumnType::Principal)))){
   for column in ["role","kind"]{if t.columns.contains_key(column){out.insert(column.into());}}
  }
  fn visit(bound:&Bound,rule:&RuleExpr,table:&str,out:&mut BTreeSet<String>){match rule{
   RuleExpr::Exists{table:t,index,key,equalities,..} if t==table=>for (column,term) in privilege::probe_pairs(bound,t,index,key,equalities){if matches!(term,RuleTerm::Literal(Value::String(_)|Value::Bool(true))){out.insert(column);}},
   RuleExpr::And(xs)|RuleExpr::Or(xs)=>for rule in xs{visit(bound,rule,table,out)},RuleExpr::Not(rule)=>visit(bound,rule,table,out),_=>{}
  }}
  for t in bound.schema.tables.values(){for rule in super::columns::rules(t){visit(bound,rule,table,&mut out);}}out
 }
 let mut authority_cache=BTreeMap::new();
 let mut candidates:BTreeMap<(String,String,String,String),bool>=BTreeMap::new();
 for doc in &catalog.documents{for p in doc.programs.iter().filter(|p|p.kind==ast::ProgramKind::Mutation){
  let operation=super::super::operation_name(std::path::Path::new(&doc.file).file_stem().and_then(|s|s.to_str()).unwrap_or(&doc.file),&p.name);
  for (write,_) in authority_walk::writes(catalog,p){
   let ast::Expr::Write{op:ast::WriteOp::Insert|ast::WriteOp::Upsert,table,fields,site,..}=write else{continue;};
   let Some(t)=bound.schema.tables.get(&table)else{continue;};
   let roles=authority_cache.entry(table.clone()).or_insert_with(||{
   let mut roles=authority_fields(bound,&table);
   for mut document in catalog.documents.clone(){super::super::intent_walk::document(&mut document,&mut |e|if let ast::Expr::Read(read)|ast::Expr::Exists{read,..}=e&&read.table==table{
    for key in &read.keys{if let Some(column)=&key.column&&matches!(key.value,ast::Expr::Literal{..}){roles.insert(column.clone());}}
   });}
   for program in catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|doc|&doc.programs){
    for (_,context) in authority_walk::writes(catalog,program){for statement in &context.body{
     if let ast::Stmt::Require{condition,..}=statement{let mut condition=condition.clone();super::super::intent_walk::expression(&mut condition,&mut |e|if let ast::Expr::Read(read)|ast::Expr::Exists{read,..}=e&&read.table==table{
      for key in &read.keys{if let Some(column)=&key.column&&matches!(key.value,ast::Expr::Literal{..}){roles.insert(column.clone());}}
     });}
    }}
   }
   roles
   });
   for (column,default) in &t.defaults{
    let ColumnDefault::Literal(default)=default else{continue;};
    if roles.contains(column)||["approv","authoriz"].iter().any(|part|column.to_lowercase().contains(part))||matches!(default,Value::Bool(true)){continue;}
    let literal=fields.iter().find(|field|&field.name==column).and_then(|field|field.value.as_ref()).and_then(|value|match value{
     ast::Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Str(value),..}=>Some(Value::String(value.clone())),
     ast::Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Bool(value),..}=>Some(Value::Bool(*value)),
     ast::Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Null,..}=>Some(Value::Null),
     ast::Expr::Literal{value:ast::Literal::Number(_),..}=>creation_advice::duration_number(value).map(Value::Int),
     _=>None
    });
    let eligible=literal.as_ref()==Some(default);
    candidates.entry((operation.clone(),table.clone(),column.clone(),site.to_string())).and_modify(|all|*all&=eligible).or_insert(eligible);
   }
  }
 }}
 for ((operation,table,column,site),eligible) in candidates{if eligible{bound.security_literal_defaults.entry((operation,table,column)).or_default().insert(site);}}
}

//! Repairs lead; uncertainty stays visible without repeating every proof blocker.
// @ref LLP 3000.036.004#144-actions-precede-coverage-details — advice is separate from replayed failures.
use super::{Report, Status};

/// Compact human questionnaire; original diagnostics remain in Report.notes.
pub fn questionnaire_notes(notes:&[crate::Diagnostic])->Vec<crate::Diagnostic>{
    let mut groups=std::collections::BTreeMap::<String,Vec<&crate::Diagnostic>>::new();
    let mut out=Vec::new();
    for n in notes{
        if !matches!(n.code.as_str(),"N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"|"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"|"N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE"){out.push(n.clone());continue;}
        let table=if n.code=="N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE"{n.message.split("write any ").nth(1).and_then(|s|s.split_whitespace().next())}
            else{n.message.split_whitespace().next().filter(|s|s.contains('.')).and_then(|s|s.split('.').next())};
        if let Some(table)=table.filter(|s|!s.is_empty()){groups.entry(table.into()).or_default().push(n);}else{out.push(n.clone());}
    }
    for (table,notes) in groups{
        let site=notes.iter().map(|n|&n.site).min_by_key(|s|(&s.file,s.line,s.col)).unwrap();
        let subjects=notes.iter().map(|n|n.message.split(": ").next().unwrap_or(&n.message).split(" matches").next().unwrap_or("").to_owned()).collect::<std::collections::BTreeSet<_>>();
        let subjects=subjects.into_iter().filter(|s|s.len()<100).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        let code=if notes.iter().any(|n|matches!(n.code.as_str(),"N_ROLE_WIDE_READ"|"N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE")){"N_TABLE_QUESTIONNAIRE"}else{"N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE"};
        out.push(crate::Diagnostic::new(site,code,format!("{table}: confirm role, row relationship, lifecycle and private-column intent ({subjects}; {} underlying questions). Answer the per-table questionnaire in snapback/generated/security-invariants.q.draft; check --json keeps every audience, source site and individual review ID",notes.len()),"keep confirmed specification intent; narrow unintended authority; record individual decisions with check --ack-security <ID> --reason '<spec rationale>'").guide("security"));
    }
    out
}


/// Potential individual gates; the CLI additionally requires current witnesses.
pub const GATED_NOTE_CODES: &[&str] = &[
    "N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT", "N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE", "N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY",
    "N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY", "N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING", "N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED", "N_BLANK_TEXT_GUARD",
];
pub fn gated_note(code: &str) -> bool { GATED_NOTE_CODES.contains(&code) }

/// One consequence ordering shared by check and audit. Lower means more urgent.
pub fn note_priority(code: &str) -> u8 {
    match code {
        "N_RETAINED_ROLE_GRANT" | "N_OPTIONAL_UPLOAD_CREATE" | "N_PRESENCE_LOOSENING" => 0,
        "N_PRIVILEGE_ESCALATION" | "N_SELF_CLAIMED_RELATIONSHIP" | "N_PRIVATE_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY" => 1,
        "N_GRANT_OUTLIVES_ROLE" | "N_OPTIONAL_EVIDENCE" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_READ" |
        "N_BROAD_READ" | "N_CALLER_TIME" | "N_SWEEP_ONLY_DEADLINE" |
        "N_CREATION_REASON" | "N_CREATION_MONEY" | "N_CONSTANT_APPROVAL" |
        "N_TRANSITION_BYPASS" | "N_STALE_CREATION_CHECK" | "N_DURATION_BOUNDS" |
        "N_TABLE_QUESTIONNAIRE" | "N_APPROVAL_INFORMATIONAL" | "N_APPROVAL_NOT_ENFORCED" | "N_EDIT_AFTER_APPROVAL" |
        "N_TEXT_EVIDENCE" | "N_MEDIA_PRIVATE_FIELDS" | "N_CHILD_EVIDENCE_PRESENCE" | "N_TIME_TRANSITION" | "N_GUARD_NOT_CONSUMED" |
        "N_CALENDAR_DURATION" | "N_DEPENDENT_WINDOW" | "N_ROLE_WIDE_WRITE" | "N_CREATE_EDIT_ASYMMETRY" | "N_EVIDENCE_REMOVAL" => 2,
        "N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY" | "N_INSERT_WRITE_INTENT" | "N_ARGUMENT_GRANT" | "N_WRITE_DENIED" | "N_PROBE_ONLY_READ" | "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE" | "N_MAINTAIN_AUTHORIZATION" => 3,
        "N_LOOKUP_FILTERED" | "N_QUERY_FILTERED" | "N_UNIQUE_HIDDEN" | "N_SPARSE_FILTER" | "N_MAINTAINED_COLUMN_DISCLOSURE" => 6,
        "N_COLUMN_RESULT_EXPOSURE" => 5,
        "N_INVARIANT_RESTATES_RULE" => 6,
        "N_SECURITY_INTENT" => 7,
        _ if gated_note(code) => 0,
        _ => 4,
    }
}

impl Report {
    /// Full structured evidence remains in the report; this is a human action list.

        let failures = self.invariants.iter().filter(|v| v.status == Status::Refuted &&
            v.counterexample.as_ref().is_some_and(|c| c.reachability.is_reachable())).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let compact=questionnaire_notes(&self.notes);
        let mut notes = compact.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        notes.sort_by_key(|n| (note_priority(&n.code), n.site.file.clone(), n.site.line, n.site.col, &n.code, &n.message));

# LLP 3000.037.000: Wave-30 default verdict and password starters

**Type:** RFC
**Status:** Implemented
**Systems:** Snapback 4 CLI, column write authority, proof kit, starters
**Author:** Charlie Cheever / Codex
**Date:** 2026-10-26
**Revised:** 2026-10-26
**Related:** LLP 3000.037, LLP 3000.036.004, LLP 3000.036.004.000, LLP 3000.011.003.000.000

## 1. Default verdict

Wave 30's 033 F1 had a concrete campaign witness, but its builder never opened
`--review`. Both default check and changed deploy search the existing bounded
question campaign and print every available unacknowledged witness with its
question. This is an actionable fix question, not an authored invariant
refutation or an additional gate. Gate semantics remain SEC10's responsibility.
Deploy retains witnessed virtual `$derived/` source sites even when ordinary
changed-source filtering cannot map them to an authored filename.
The check verdict and deploy’s leading verdict count gates, witnessed questions and advisories;
advisories explicitly do not block. Unwitnessed advice remains behind --review.
N_WRITE_DENIED, N_REQUIRE_HIDDEN, N_NEVER_COMPLETE and N_UNREADABLE_PROBE are
an actionable tier shown by default. The expanded security-question block leads with gates, then
witnessed questions, then other advice. A disposition lacking a witness says
that its specification rationale has not been validated by replay. Unsupported
codes say witness unavailable; supported exhausted searches say none obtained.
Neither wording promises that the acknowledged specification is true.

Existing redaction, source binding, work caps and structured evidence survive.
The existing generated cache is an optimization, never authority. A cold check
now pays the bounded witness search; measurements must include cold latency.
No witness is not evidence of safety. Default output targets median <=5 lines
for both check and deploy on the fifty wave-30 schemas; individual actionable
findings can exceed it. Deploy prints its newline-terminated security notice
without adding a blank line. Enforcement prose and JSON evidence remain intact.

## 2. Default initialization

LLP 3000.036.004.000 §10 supersedes its original "defaults count" rule.
A null or a value equal to a declared literal default initializes a column at
insert and requires no column write grant. Explicitly supplying the same
literal is equivalent to omission. This applies to insert-if-absent and the
insert arm of upsert. Table insert authority always applies. A non-default
insert value requires the grant. Changed updates, including resetting a value
to its default and edits via upsert, retain old-and-next grant checks.
Dynamic `now` and `viewer` defaults retain ordinary column authority; this
amendment grants only literal initialization, with no new wire expression.
Authored column-write intent and enforced adversarial contracts use that same
initialization predicate.
Authored whole-row presence/approval assertions remain independent obligations.

## 3. Password online starters

`init --web --online --password` and the Expo equivalent create a small notes
app with password sign-in, operator-only provision, online tables and writes,
no device initialization, no personas, and no todo fixtures. `--password`
also selects this shape for a new schema without requiring --online. Beside an
existing schema init preserves it and validates an explicit password request.
The example's real member/admin accounts are provisioned before frontend proofs;
new public signups have no role and cannot create notes until provisioned.
Custom schemas still require application-specific write proofs.

Password clients retain registerWebWitness through adaptation. Exact pre-DX13
password client snapshots remain recognized, including no-sync transforms;
application-owned edits still prevent replacement. Starter detection
recognizes starter metadata or launcher/client wiring, independent of optional
probe scripts and package-manager version spellings. Generated UI starters offer
an install wrapper with streaming package output and a ten-second elapsed-time
heartbeat; it preserves npm's exit status. It does not automatically install. Exact README recognition canonicalizes the
legacy npm command and the generated helper command; adaptation emits the helper
command only where its file exists, retaining custom prose.

## 4. Proof diagnostics and account plans

serve-check compares its requested deployment with path-only observations of
startServe in its owned script group. These observations contain no credentials
and are disposed after cleanup. A mismatch names both stores, including when
the requested store exists and is unlocked. A missing store is distinct from
a live lock, and a live lock names the resolved path and holder from the CLI's
ownership report. Other barrier failures remain explicitly unknown. No process
is signalled based on these observations. Raw script stderr and readiness stay
private. Direct arbitrary native scripts without kit observations can only
report the requested barrier's path and holder; no actual-path claim is invented.

Account plans optionally add `users` roleless accounts beside `roles`; they
return under sessions.users and never call provision. users-only plans need no
provision handle or service token. Named role `users` conflicts with this batch
and refuses. The 100-account combined cap and host-environment validation remain.
E_STAGING_PROVISION prints merged argument shapes with opaque values redacted,
and declared mutation parameter names, never passwords, tokens or raw payloads.
Credential snapshots use unique mode-0600 files so rerunning disposable staging
recipes cannot fail merely because a previous snapshot exists.

## 5. Evidence and reviews

The workstream report records affected checks, packed starter/guide execution,
fifty-schema output and hole visibility measurements, copied friction replays,
and two independent reviews. Source applications are read-only; replay stores
live under /tmp/dx13-replay and are removed. Mac is explicitly out of scope.
This work neither publishes nor changes the package version.

    std::fs::write(project.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),source).unwrap();let sources=project.sources().unwrap();
    let bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&sources);assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty());let options=Options{positionals:Vec::new(),flags:BTreeMap::new(),json:false};

    let decisions=super::super::security_reviews::review_evidence(&project,&sources,&report.notes,None,&evidence).unwrap();

        let witness=decision.witness.as_ref().unwrap();let kind=if matches!(witness["effect"].as_str(),Some("insert"|"upsert")){"audience: creators deliberately have broader authority"}else{"intended effect: the observed edit is deliberate"};
        let reason=format!("witness {}; permit {} as {}; {}; operation {} is explicitly intended",witness["id"].as_str().unwrap(),witness["operation"].as_str().unwrap(),witness["viewer"].as_str().unwrap(),kind,witness["operation"].as_str().unwrap());
        super::super::security_reviews::review_evidence(&project,&sources,&report.notes,Some((&decision.id,&reason)),&evidence).unwrap();reviewed+=1;
    }
    assert!(reviewed>=1,"the actual production witness has an individual decision");
    let notice=release_loop(&project,&bound,&sources,None,&options).unwrap().unwrap();
    assert!(!notice.contains("  FIX "),"acknowledged candidates must not remain outstanding FIX: {notice}");
    let changed_source=source.replace("update docs[doc] {value}","update docs[doc] {value: value + 1}");
    std::fs::write(project.schema_dir().join("schema.q"),&changed_source).unwrap();let changed=project.sources().unwrap();let changed_bound=snapback4_lang::compile(&changed);
    assert!(enforce_release(release_loop(&project,&changed_bound,&changed,None,&options).unwrap().as_ref(),&options).is_err(),"a changed rule needs a new decision");
}

#[test]fn fresh_deploy_replays_nested_sibling_approval_without_authored_intent(){

    86	pub(super) fn validate_reason(reason:&str,value:&serde_json::Value)->Result<(),String>{
    87	    if !concrete(value){return Ok(());}
    88	    let witness=id(value);let operation=value["operation"].as_str().unwrap();

codex
**NOT READY**

- **P1 — Derived witnesses occupy both blocking and nonblocking tiers.** [security.rs:142](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/security.rs:142) counts unacknowledged concrete derived refutations as production-blocking FIXes. Their `N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY` dispositions are nongated, so those same witnesses are counted as questions and rendered with “doesn’t block” by [decisions.rs:87](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/security_reviews/decisions.rs:87). Production then refuses because `fixes > 0`. Rendering, counts, and refusal need one disjoint classification; nongating inferred questions must remain nonblocking.

- **P2 — Compact presentation now changes witness identity.** [witnesses.rs:147](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/security/witnesses.rs:147) adds `compactSummary` before stamping, but canonicalization excludes only `summary` and other existing presentation fields. Thus identical evidence gains a different digest solely from the new display field, invalidating existing acknowledgments. Generated IDs embedded inside this summary string also escape typed ID normalization. Exclude `compactSummary` from canonical witness identity.

These findings concern the authored composition seam. Source inspection only; no files changed or agents spawned.

**NOT READY**

- **P1 — Derived witnesses occupy both blocking and nonblocking tiers.** [security.rs:142](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/security.rs:142) counts unacknowledged concrete derived refutations as production-blocking FIXes. Their `N_DERIVED_WRITE_AUTHORITY` dispositions are nongated, so those same witnesses are counted as questions and rendered with “doesn’t block” by [decisions.rs:87](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/security_reviews/decisions.rs:87). Production then refuses because `fixes > 0`. Rendering, counts, and refusal need one disjoint classification; nongating inferred questions must remain nonblocking.

- **P2 — Compact presentation now changes witness identity.** [witnesses.rs:147](/home/ccheever/projects/snapback-sb4loop/snapback4/crates/snapback4/src/verbs/security/witnesses.rs:147) adds `compactSummary` before stamping, but canonicalization excludes only `summary` and other existing presentation fields. Thus identical evidence gains a different digest solely from the new display field, invalidating existing acknowledgments. Generated IDs embedded inside this summary string also escape typed ID normalization. Exclude `compactSummary` from canonical witness identity.

These findings concern the authored composition seam. Source inspection only; no files changed or agents spawned.
